# Humanidad

La humanidad o el camino a la iluminacion es lo que evita que el vampiro caiga o sea dominado por la bestia. Aunque existen multiples tipos de caminos que han sido creados como ideales algunos casi religiosos sobre los que mantenerse para evitar a la bestia, el camino de la humanidad es el mas comun de todos.

Los caminos a la iluminacion se dividen en 10 niveles, y por regla general todos los vampiros empiezan en el 7mo, teniendo efectivamente 7/10 humanidad.

Distintas acciones variando segun el camino que uno elija ayudan a que esto suba o baje, de manera muy similar a estres/gracia o voluntad, solo que aparte, mucho mas esoterico, y ligado estrictamente a la bestia que todo vampiro lleva dentro.

Tener mas o menos de 7 tiene sus efectos, en algunos caminos pueden variar al menos para arriba, aunque para abajo rara vez cambian. Aca pondre los del camino de la humanidad al ser el mas comun, si quieren algo unico se lo puede charlar y ver e investigar.

**Humanidad:**

**0:** Wight. Monstruo, la bestia. Te has perdido por completo, ya no hay vuelta atras, ya no eres mas que un cascaron vacio controlado completamente por la bestia, comer y dormir es lo unico que sabes hacer. game over.

**1:** Horrorifico. Eres alguien al borde de la perdicion, practicamente un monstruo, con un aspecto cadaverico y un deseo insano de alimentarte de manera casi constante. Cualquier cosa podria hacerte caer para siempre. Tienes +18 DC extra para resistir el frenzy.

**2:** Bestial. fancy description me dio paja. tienes +15 DC extra para resistir el frenzy.

**3:** +12 DC extra para resistir el frenzy.

**4:** +9 DC extra para resistir el frenzy.

**5:** +6 DC extra para resistir el frenzy.

**6:** +3 DC extra para resistir el frenzy.

**7:** Normal.

**8:** 3 DC menos para resistir el frenzy.

**9:** 5 DC menos para resistir el frenzy.

**10:** 7 DC menos para resistir el frenzy, posibilidad de alcanzar golconda la iluminacion trascender, el nirvana, etc, como pija quieras llamarlo.

Mythic Path: Ninguno pero todos. No se elige un path.

Mythic Tier/lvl: Cada nivel mitico te otorga 2 “puntos miticos” que podes gastar de distintas formas, cada path ability, mytic feat o demas cosas tienen costes diferentes, por regla general 1 valen las mythic feats, y entre 2 y 3 las path abilities.

Mythic Power: El "mana" mitico digamosle, aquello que tienen que gastar a la hora de usar la mayoria de capacidades miticas. Tienen 2 + nivel mitico.

Mythic Path Abilities: Podes elegir de cualquier path. (acorde al rol puede que haya cierta limitacion según la fuente de su poder mitico) Se respetan aun asi el requisito de tier. Si estan en la lista de poderes de tercer tier mitico, tienen que esperar a tener 3 nivel mitico o no podran ponersela.

\[1\]: Mythic feat o path ability muy debil que solo cuesta 1 punto mitico ponerse.

\[2\]: Path ability de buen poder que cuesta 2 puntos miticos ponerse.

\[3\]: Path ability seguramente rota, negrero de mierda, cuesta 3 puntos miticos ponersela.

#### 1st-Tier Universal Path Abilities

You can select these path abilities at any tier.

##### Force of Will (Ex) \[1\]

You can exert your will to force events to unfold as you would like. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to reroll a d20 roll you just made, or force any non-mythic creature to reroll a d20 roll it just made. You can use this ability after the results are revealed. Whoever rerolls a roll must take the result of the second roll, even if it is lower.

##### Mythic Saving Throws (Ex) \[2\]

Whenever you succeed at a saving throw against a spell or special ability, you suffer no effects as long as that ability didn’t come from a mythic source (such as a creature with a mythic tier or mythic ranks). If you fail a saving throw that results from a mythic source, you take the full effects as normal.

##### Recuperation (Ex) \[2\]

You are restored to full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) after 8 hours of rest so long as you aren’t [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead). In addition, by expending one use of mythic power and resting for 1 hour, you regain a number of [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) equal to half your full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) (up to a maximum of your full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points)) and regain the use of any class features that are limited to a certain number of uses per day (such as [<u>barbarian</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian) rage, [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance), spells per day, and so on). This rest is treated as 8 hours of sleep for such abilities. This rest doesn’t refresh uses of mythic power or any mythic abilities that are limited to a number of times per day.

##### Surge (Su) \[1\]

You can call upon your mythic power to overcome difficult challenges. You can expend one use of mythic power to increase any d20 roll you just made by rolling 1d6 and adding it to the result. Using this ability is an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) taken after the result of the original roll is revealed. This can change the outcome of the roll. The bonus die gained by using this ability increases to 1d8 at 4th tier, 1d10 at 7th tier, and 1d12 at 10th tier.

##### Hard to Kill (Ex) \[1\]

Whenever you’re below 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), you automatically [<u>stabilize</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stable) without needing to attempt a [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) check. If you have an ability that allows you to act while below 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), you still lose [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) for taking actions, as specified by that ability. Bleed damage still causes you to lose [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) when below 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points). In addition, you don’t die until your total number of negative [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) is equal to or greater than double your [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) score.

##### Commune with Power (Su) \[1\]

You can tap into your mythic nature to seek answers to the quandaries you encounter in your adventures. Once per day, in a special ritual that requires you to spend 1 hour of uninterrupted meditation, you can commune with the source of your power. This acts as the [*<u>commune</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/commune) spell, using your tier as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level). The source of your power may be limited in the knowledge it possesses or can deliver, as decided upon by the GM.

##### Display of [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores/#Charisma_Cha) (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a feat of [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-), gaining a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on one [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-)-based skill check or [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) ability check.

##### Display of Constitution (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a feat of [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-), gaining a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on one [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) ability check. Alternatively, you can use this ability to apply a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) to your [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) score for a number of hours equal to your mythic tier for the purpose of making [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) checks against [<u>heat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Heat-Ex-), cold, [<u>fatigue</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued), and [<u>exhaustion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Exhausted).

##### Display of Dexterity (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a feat of [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-), gaining a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on one [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-)-based skill check or [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) ability check.

##### Display of [<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores/#Intelligence_Int) (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a feat of [<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Intelligence-Int-), gaining a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on one [<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Intelligence-Int-)-based skill check or [<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Intelligence-Int-) ability check.

##### Display of Strength (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a feat of [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-), gaining a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on one [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-)-based skill check or [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) ability check. Alternatively, you can use this ability to apply a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) to your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) score for a number of hours equal to your mythic tier for the purpose of determining your carrying capacity.

##### Display of Wisdom (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a feat of [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-), gaining a +20 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on one [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-)-based skill check or [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-) ability check.

##### Extra Mythic Power (Su) \[1\]

You can call upon your mythic power two additional times per day. You can select this ability up to three times.

##### Legendary Item (Ex) \[3\]

You gain a legendary item. This item grants a number of abilities equal to your tier (maximum 3). At 3rd tier, you can select this ability again, increasing the maximum to six abilities and causing the item to become a lesser artifact. At 6th tier, you can select this ability again, increasing the maximum to 10 abilities; the item then becomes a greater artifact.

##### Longevity (Su) \[1\]

Upon taking this ability, you can no longer die from old age. If you have penalties to your physical ability scores due to aging, you no longer take those penalties. You still continue to age, and you gain all the benefits to your mental ability scores.

##### Mythic Craft (Ex) \[1\]

When you use the [<u>Craft</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/craft) skill to create an item, you double the progress each check provides. In addition, you can make an item masterwork simply by paying for the cost, and don’t need to increase the time to create the item or attempt additional checks. Add your tier to any skill checks associated with making magic items.

##### Mythic Spellcasting (Ex) \[2\]

You can learn a number of mythic spells equal to your tier and can expend mythic power when casting them to enhance the results. To select a mythic spell, you must be able to cast the non-mythic version or have it on your list of spells known. Every time you gain a new tier, you can select an additional mythic spell. You can take this ability up to three times. Each additional time you take it, you can select an additional number of spells equal to your tier and you gain one additional mythic spell whenever you gain a tier.

##### Mythic Sustenance (Su) \[1\]

Your mythic power is enough to sustain you. You no longer need to eat, drink, or breathe to live. You’re immune to inhaled poisons and any spell or effect that requires breathing, though you are still affected by any food or drink you intake.

##### Pierce the Darkness (Sp) \[1\]

You gain permanent [<u>darkvision</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Darkvision) with a 60-foot range, as the universal monster ability. If you possess [<u>darkvision</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Darkvision), the range increases by 60 feet.

##### Binding Ritual (Su) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>PC:CaC</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601257341/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601257341&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20&linkId=2OH3I6T3B5EINNH6)

You have learned the eldritch rituals required to draw creatures from the planes into your presence. If you are a spellcaster, you add the following spells to your list of spells known: [*<u>binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/binding), [*<u>dimensional anchor</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dimensional-anchor), [*<u>magic circle against chaos</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-circle-against-chaos), [*<u>magic circle against evil</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-circle-against-evil), [*<u>magic circle against good</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-circle-against-good), [*<u>magic circle against law</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-circle-against-law), [*<u>planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding), [*<u>planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding) (greater), and [*<u>planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding) (lesser).

If these spells are not normally on your class list, you add them to your list of spells known at the level they appear on the [<u>sorcerer/wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/spell-lists-and-domains/spell-lists-sorcerer-and-wizard/) spell list, though you do not gain spells whose level exceeds the level of spells your class can cast.

Whether or not you are a spellcaster, you can expend one use of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to create a magic circle (of any type) as part of a [<u>calling</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Calling) diagram; if you expend an additional use of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power), this diagram is fortified with [*<u>dimensional anchor</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dimensional-anchor). If you are at least 9th level, you may also spend two uses of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to cast lesser [*<u>planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding) as a [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-). If you are at least 11th level and 6th tier, you can expend three uses of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to cast [*<u>planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding) as a [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-).

If you are at least 15th level and 9th tier, you can expend four uses of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to cast [*<u>greater planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding) as a [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-). For all these effects, treat your [<u>mythic tier</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Tier) as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level), unless your own [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) is higher.

##### Beyond Morality (Ex) \[1\]

You have no [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment). You can become a member of any class, even one with an [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) requirement, and can never lose your membership because of a change in [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment). If you violate the code of ethics of any of your classes, you might still lose access to certain features of such classes, subject to GM discretion. Attempts to detect your [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) don’t return any results. If a class restricts you from casting spells with an [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) descriptor, you can cast such spells without restrictions or repercussions. If you’re the target of a spell or effect that is based on [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment), you’re treated as the most favorable [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) when determining the spell’s effect on you. Any effects that alter [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) have no effect on you. If you lose this effect, you revert to your previous [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment).

##### Tongues (Sp) \[1\]

You can understand and speak any language, as the [*<u>tongues</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/tongues) spell. If this ability is dispelled, you can resume it as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions).

#### 3rd-Tier Universal Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Divine Source (Su) \[2\]

You can grant divine spells to those who follow your cause, allowing them to select you as their deity for the purposes of determining their spells and domains. Select two domains upon taking this ability. You grant access to these domains as if you were a deity. Creatures that gain spells from you don’t receive any spells per day of levels higher than your tier; they lose those spell slots. In addition, you can cast spells from domains you grant as long as their level is equal to or less than your tier. Each day as a [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-), you can cast one spell of each level equal to or less than your tier (selecting from those available to you from your divine source domains). If you’re a [<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) or you venerate a deity, you may change your spell domains to those you grant others. At 6th tier and 9th tier, you can select this ability again, adding one domain and two subdomains to your list each time and adding their spells to the list of those that you can cast.

##### Enhanced Ability (Ex) \[1\]

You gain a permanent +2 bonus to one ability score of your choice. You can select this path ability up to six times. Each time you do, it must apply to a different ability score.

##### Fearless (Su) \[1\]

Few things in the world still inspire fear in you. You are immune to all fear effects from non-mythic sources. [<u>Fear</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Fear-Su-or-Sp-) effects from mythic sources count as mythic for the purposes of this ability.

##### Interplanar Prestige (Ex, Sp) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>PC:CaC</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601257341/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601257341&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20&linkId=2OH3I6T3B5EINNH6)

Your mighty accomplishments have drawn the notice of creatures beyond your home plane. You gain a bonus equal to half your [<u>mythic tier</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Tier) on [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) checks against [<u>outsiders</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) with an [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) subtype that matches at least one portion of your [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment), and on [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) checks against [<u>outsiders</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) with an [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) subtype that opposes at least one portion of your [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment). If you have the [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) feat, you add half your [<u>mythic tier</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Tier) to your [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) score for the purpose of attracting an [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) [<u>cohort</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership).

In addition, if you are at least 7th level, you can expend two uses of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to cast [*<u>lesser planar ally</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-ally) as a [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-), treating your [<u>mythic tier</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Tier) as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level). If you are at least 11th level and 6th tier, you can expend three uses of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to cast [*<u>planar ally</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-ally).

If you are at least 15th level and 9th tier, you can expend four uses of your [<u>mythic power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/alternative-rule-systems/mythic#TOC-Mythic-Power) to cast [*<u>greater planar ally</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-ally).

You must provide the required offering to bargain for your ally’s service.

##### Pure Body (Su) \[1\]

Your body is so pure that you’re immune to all non-mythic diseases and poisons. Diseases and poisons from mythic sources count as mythic for the purposes of this ability.

##### Pure Destiny (Su) \[2\]

Your mythic destiny is guided by providence. You’re immune to all non-mythic curses and compulsions. Curses and compulsions from mythic sources count as mythic for the purposes of this ability.

##### Pure Senses (Su) \[1\]

Your senses are extraordinarily keen. You’re immune to blindness and deafness caused by non-mythic sources. Such effects from mythic sources count as mythic for the purposes of this ability.

##### Sleepless (Su) \[1\]

You no longer require sleep, don’t become [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued) or [<u>exhausted</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Exhausted) from lack of sleep, and are immune to sleep effects. If you have abilities or class features that require rest before they can be regained, you can choose to regain them once per day by spending 1 hour in uninterrupted meditation.

##### Ultimate Versatility (Ex) \[1\]

Once per day, you can temporarily change one decision made for one of your class features. This change lasts for a number of minutes equal to your tier. During this time, you’re treated as if you had always had the new class feature. For example, you could use this ability to change the decision made with the arcane bond class feature, causing your bonded item to disappear (along with all of its bonuses and restrictions) and a [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard#TOC-Arcane-Bond-Ex-or-Sp-) to appear in its place. This doesn’t affect any prepared spells or spells you have already cast. If the new ability is limited in its uses per day, you receive half the normal number of uses (minimum 1). When this ability ends, your previous choice returns with the same number of uses as before you used this ability. If you use this ability to change a class feature that grants access to spells (such as a [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline), patron, domain, or school), you lose access to any spells from the old choice but gain the ability to cast new spells. When the effect ends, the previous spells return and can be cast again. You can use this ability to change a feat or skill if you receive it from a class feature, but any other abilities that rely on the missing feat or skill as a prerequisite don’t function while this ability is in effect.

##### Unchanging (Su) \[1\]

Your form is so [<u>stable</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stable) that you become immune to non-mythic [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) and petrification effects. [*<u>Polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) and petrification from mythic sources count as mythic for the purposes of this ability.

##### Farwalker (Sp) \[2\]

The boundaries of the [<u>Material Plane</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/environment/the-planes#TOC-Material-Plane1) mean little to you. You can [*<u>plane shift</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/plane-shift) two times per day, using double your tier as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level). You can take this ability twice. When you take it a second time, you can use this ability two extra times per day.

##### Mythic Sight (Sp) \[2\]

When you take this ability, you gain [<u>blinds</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Blindsight-and-Blindsense)ight out to a range of 30 feet. You can take this ability twice. The second time you take it, increases its range by 60 feet. This ability doesn’t apply to illusions and magical effects that were cast by other mythic creatures or that are affecting other mythic creatures. If this ability is dispelled, you can resume it as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions).

#### 6th-Tier Universal Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Mythic Presence (Su) \[2\]

Your power unnerves ordinary mortals. By expending one use of mythic power, you gain an effect similar to [<u>frightful presence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Frightful-Presence-Ex-), except that creatures are affected according to their tier (or rank, in the case of monsters) instead of their [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-). Non-mythic creatures that fail their saves are [<u>panicked</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Panicked) for 1 minute, and those that succeed are [<u>shaken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Shaken) for 1 minute. Creatures with a mythic tier or rank equal to or less than your tier are unaffected if they succeed, and are [<u>shaken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Shaken) for 1 minute if they fail. Creatures with a mythic tier or rank higher than yours are unaffected by your mythic presence. The [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save against your mythic presence equals 10 + your tier + your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier. This ability lasts for 1 minute and has a range of 30 feet.

#### 1st-Tier Archmage Path Abilities

##### Arcane Surge (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to cast any one arcane spell without expending a prepared spell or spell slot. If you prepare spells, this spell must be one you prepared today (even if you have already cast it); if you’re a spontaneous caster, this spell must be one of your spells known. If the spell requires a saving throw, any non-mythic creatures affected by the spell roll twice and take the lower result.

If you must attempt a [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) check for the spell to overcome a creature’s [<u>spell resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Spell-Resistance), you can roll your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) check twice (adding your tier to each) and take the higher result. You can’t add a [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) to a spell you cast using this ability.

##### Mage Strike (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to make one melee attack. This is in addition to any other attacks you make this round. If you expend one arcane spell that you’ve prepared or arcane spell slot that you have available, you gain a bonus on the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) equal to double the spell’s or slot’s level, and the attack deals an additional 2d6 points of energy damage (your choice of acid, cold, electricity, or fire) per level of the spell or slot expended. The normal damage from this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction), but the energy damage is still affected by resistances and immunities.

##### Wild Arcana (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend two uses of mythic powers to cast any one arcane spell without expending a prepared spell or spell slot. The spell must be on one of your arcane class spell lists and must be of a level that you can cast with that arcane spellcasting class.

You don’t need to have the spell prepared, nor does it need to be on your list of spells known. When casting a spell in this way, you treat your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) as 2 levels higher for the purpose of any effect dependent on level. You can apply any [<u>metamagic feats</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) you know to this spell, but its total adjusted level can’t be greater than that of the highest-level arcane spell you can cast from that spellcasting class.

##### Abundant Casting (Ex) \[1\]

Whenever you cast a spell that specifically targets a limited number of targets (such as “one creature/level” or “one or more creatures”), add your tier to the number of targets the spell can affect. For example, a 5th-level [<u>wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard)/1st-tier archmage can target up to six creatures when casting [*<u>haste</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/haste) (five from [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) 5th and one from 1st tier). Alternatively, you can expend one use of mythic power when casting a spell that affects only one target (such as “one creature”) to have the spell affect an additional target. If the spell requires you to succeed at a melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks), you must hold the charge in order to make a second [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks).

##### Arcane Endurance (Ex) \[1\]

Your spells always count as Extended.

##### Bloodline Intensity (Su) \[1\]

Your mythic power intensifies your [<u>sorcerer bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline). Select an additional bloodline feat from your [<u>bloodline’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) list of feats. You must meet the prerequisites for that feat. You can also cast each of your [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) spells once per day without them counting toward the number of spells you can cast per day. You must have the [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) class feature to select this ability.

##### Competent Caster (Ex) \[2\]

You automatically succeed at [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) checks to cast arcane spells. This ability doesn’t apply to spells of the highest spell level you can cast.

##### Coupled Arcana (Ex) \[1\]

Whenever you spend a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions), or [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) to [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) an arcane school power, [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance), [<u>bloodline power</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline), hex, or [<u>magus arcana</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/magus#TOC-Magus-Arcana), you can also [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) an ability that uses mythic power as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions). The mythic ability you [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) must require the same action type as the other ability you activated or faster (a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) is faster than a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions), and a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions) is faster than a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions)).

##### Crafting Mastery (Ex) \[2\]

You can craft any magic item as if you had the necessary [<u>item creation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/item-creation-feats) feats. If you actually have the [<u>item creation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/item-creation-feats) feat needed for a magic item you’re crafting, whenever you attempt a skill check to create that item, roll twice and use the higher result, and you make twice as much progress on the item for any time spent. This ability does not reduce the item’s cost or any other requirements.

##### Deep Understanding (Ex) \[1\]

You automatically identify any arcane spell cast within 60 feet of you if it’s on your class’s spell list and you’re of a high enough level to cast it. Whenever you attempt to identify a non-artifact magic item using [*<u>detect magic</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/detect-magic), you automatically learn its properties and command words in the first round of the spell (no [<u>Spellcraft</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/spellcraft) roll needed), but you can’t automatically determine whether it’s cursed.

##### Eldritch Breach (Su) \[1\]

You are adept at breaching magical defenses and overcoming resistance to your magic. When attempting a [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) check to dispel an effect, overcome [<u>spell resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Spell-Resistance), or otherwise determine whether your magic affects a target (such as with [*<u>knock</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/k/knock) or [*<u>neutralize poison</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/n/neutralize-poison)), roll twice and take the higher result.

##### Elemental Bond (Su) \[2\]

You are connected to one of the elemental planes. Select one [<u>elemental plane</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/environment/the-planes#TOC-Elemental-Planes): air, earth, fire, or water. Whenever you cast a spell with a descriptor matching that plane, add your tier to your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) for that spell. You gain resistance 10 against an energy type associated with your chosen plane—electricity for air, acid for earth, fire for fire, and cold for water. At 6th tier, this resistance increases to 20. At 9th tier, this [<u>resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Energy-Resistance) increases to 30.

##### Enduring Armor (Su) \[1\]

You are protected by armor made of force. This armor grants you an [<u>armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus) to AC equal to 3 + your tier. This ability is an [<u>abjuration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Abjuration) effect with a spell level equal to your tier. If this armor is dispelled or otherwise ended, you can reactivate it as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions).

##### Energy Conversion (Su) \[2\]

Whenever you cast a spell with the acid, cold, electricity, or [<u>fire descriptor</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Fire), you can expend one use of mythic power to switch the energy type to a different one of those energy types. If the spell normally has its original energy type as a descriptor, it loses that descriptor and gains the new type as a descriptor. All other effects of the spell remain unchanged.

##### Enhance Magic Items (Ex) \[1\]

Your mythic presence enhances the power of certain magic items. Add half your tier to the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) of [*<u>potions</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions), [*<u>scrolls</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/scrolls), staves, and [*<u>wands</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands) you use. When using a staff or [*<u>wand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands), you may [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) the item by expending one use of mythic power instead of one of the item’s charges.

##### Flash of Omniscience (Ex) \[1\]

Your ability to recall information is astounding. When you take 10 on any [<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Intelligence-Int-)-based skill check, add your tier to the result. As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to ask one question as if using divination. The base chance for a correct answer is 70% + your highest arcane [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) + your tier (maximum 100%). The answer doesn’t come from a divine agent, but from your own native understanding; therefore, the answer is never cryptic. If you don’t get a correct answer, it’s like failing a [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) check, and you can’t use this ability on this question again.

##### Flexible Counterspell (Su) \[1\]

Your mythic power enhances your ability to counter others’ spells. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt to [<u>counter</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) a spell. This ability otherwise works like readying an action to [<u>counter</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) a spell, except instead of using the exact spell or [*<u>dispel magic</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dispel-magic), you can instead expend a spell or spell slot of a level equal to or higher than the target spell.

##### Flexible School (Ex) \[1\]

Select one [<u>wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard) arcane school that is not your arcane school. You gain powers from that arcane school, treating your tier as your [<u>wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard) level for the purposes of these powers. Once you have chosen the arcane school, it cannot be changed. You cannot select an arcane school that is one of your opposition schools.

You must have the arcane school class feature to select this ability. You can choose this ability up to three times, each time selecting another arcane school other than your own.

##### Greater Familiar Link (Su) \[2\]

You have bound yourself so deeply to your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) that your life forces are intertwined. Anytime you or your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) takes damage, you may split the damage evenly between you and your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) as if using [*<u>shield other</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/shield-other). Using this ability doesn’t require an action, but it works only if you’re conscious and both you and your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) are alive.

You must have a [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard#TOC-Arcane-Bond-Ex-or-Sp-) to select this ability.

##### Harmonious Mage (Ex) \[1\]

Your wizardly studies have moved beyond the concept of opposition schools. Select two opposition schools. These are no longer opposition schools for you, preparing spells from one of these schools now only requires one spell slot of the appropriate level instead of two, and you no longer take the –4 [<u>Spellcraft</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/spellcraft) penalty for crafting items from an opposition school.

You must have the arcane school class ability to select this ability, and your arcane school can’t be universal.

##### Mythic Bloodline (Su) \[1\]

When determining the effects of your [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) powers, you’re considered 4 levels higher. This increases the effects of powers you have access to, but doesn’t grant you powers at a lower level than normal (aunque si el poder es narrativamente interesante y no mecanicamente roto si lo permito). If you have a [<u>sorcerer bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) power that can be used multiple times per day, such as metamagic adept, you gain a number of extra uses of that power equal to half your tier (minimum 1). You must have the [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) class feature to select this ability.

##### Mythic Hexes (Su) \[2\]

Your [<u>hexes</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/witch#TOC-Hex) are more effective against non-mythic targets. When you use a hex that requires a saving throw against a non-mythic target, that target is automatically affected for 1 round (which doesn’t count toward the hex’s duration) and can’t attempt a saving throw to resist the hex. On your turn the next round, the creature attempts its saving throw as normal.

If it succeeds at this saving throw, it breaks free of the hex; otherwise, it’s affected as normal. You must have the hex class feature to select this ability. You can select this ability twice. If you select it a second time, it also affects your major [<u>hexes</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/witch#TOC-Hex).

##### Mythic School (Su) \[1\]

When determining the effects of your arcane school powers, you’re considered 4 levels higher. This increases the effects of powers you have access to, but doesn’t grant you powers at a lower level than normal. If you have an arcane school power that can be used multiple times per day, such as [*<u>blinding ray</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/blinding-ray), you gain a number of extra uses of that power equal half your tier (minimum 1). You must have the arcane school class feature to select this ability.

##### Perfect Preparation (Ex) \[1\]

You have discovered the secret to preparing spells without having to refer to outside sources. You no longer need to prepare spells from a spellbook (if you’re a [<u>magus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/magus) or [<u>wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard)) or a [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard#TOC-Arcane-Bond-Ex-or-Sp-) (if you’re a [<u>witch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/witch)). You still must spend the normal amount of time preparing spells. You may keep or discard your spellbook or [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar).

##### Rapid Preparation (Su) \[1\]

You can prepare a spell in an open spell slot in only 1 minute instead of the normal 15 minutes. You can prepare spells in all of your available spell slots in only 15 minutes instead of the normal 1 hour. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to instantly prepare one spell in an open spell slot.

##### Remixer (Ex) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You can instantly remix any [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) you touch to alter its effects. You can expend one use of mythic power to change the spell or formula contained in a [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) or extract to that of any spell or extract you have prepared of the same spell level. The resulting [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) uses the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) of the original [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions).

The new spell contained in the [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) or elixir must be one that could normally serve as a [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions). If the effect you are trying to use requires expensive material [<u>components</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Components), you must provide them at the time of the change. The DC of a [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) check to successfully [*<u>identify</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/i/identify) the powers of the [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) (normally equal to 15 + the [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions)‘s [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level)) increases by an amount equal to your tier.

##### Resilient Arcana (Su) \[1\]

Your spells and effects become more difficult to identify and dispel. Add your tier to the DC of any checks to identify your spells, recognize them as they are being cast, or dispel them using [*<u>dispel magic</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dispel-magic) or similar effects.

##### Sensory Link (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can see and hear through your [<u>familiar’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) eyes and ears. While using this ability, you can’t see or hear using your normal senses. You can speak through your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) using any language you know, even if the [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) can’t normally speak. You may end the link and restore your own senses as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions). While linked, you can expend one use of mythic power to cast a spell and have it originate from your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) instead of you. You must have a [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard#TOC-Arcane-Bond-Ex-or-Sp-) to select this ability.

##### Shapeshifting Mastery (Ex) \[2\]

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

Your ability to magically adopt other forms is unparalleled, and you can expertly translate your arcane might into brawn. You add half your tier to the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) of spells or extracts from the [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) subschool. While under the effects of a spell or extract of the [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) subschool, you can use your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) instead of your [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) when making [<u>natural attacks</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks) that rely on your new form.

##### Shifting Mastery (Su) \[1\]

Whenever you cast or use an arcane [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) spell, you can grant the target the ability to expend 1 minute of the spell’s duration to assume another form allowed by the spell. Changing form in this way is a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions). The target can assume another form as often as it likes for the duration of the spell. If you’re the target, you can change forms as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) rather than a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions). If you expend one use of mythic power when you cast the spell, you can also grant the target the ability to assume the shape of one specific individual that the spell could duplicate (for example, the target could use [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) to assume the form of a specific [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal), [<u>humanoid</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Humanoid), or [<u>elemental</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Elemental), but not a [<u>dragon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Dragon) because [<u>dragon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Dragon) forms aren’t allowed by [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph)). This specific creature must be known to you, but it doesn’t have to be known by the target. The target gains a +10 bonus on [<u>Disguise</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/disguise) checks to pass as that individual, but [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff) checks or knowledge of the individual might be required to make the disguise truly convincing.

##### Spellbane Counterstrike (Su) \[1\]

With a quick sprint, you retaliate against the caster of a spell you countered. When you successfully [<u>counter</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) a spell, you can move up to your speed as part of the [<u>counterspell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) action, as long as you end your movement with the target of the [<u>counterspell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) within melee reach. Your movement from this ability doesn’t count toward your total movement for the round.

If the target’s casting would have provoked an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), you can immediately take an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) against the target.

##### Telekinetic Master (Su) \[1\]

You can use [*<u>mage hand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mage-hand) or open/close at will as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), and you can affect unattended magical objects with [*<u>mage hand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mage-hand). The weight limit for these abilities increases by 5 pounds per tier.

Treat your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) as 2 levels higher when casting [*<u>levitate</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/l/levitate), [*<u>telekinesis</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/telekinesis), and similar spells.

##### Throw Spell (Su) \[1\]

When casting a melee [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) spell, you can expend one use of mythic power to throw that spell as a ranged [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks). The range of this thrown spell is 10 feet per tier. If the spell allows you to make multiple melee touch attacks, you may make any number of the attacks as melee [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) attacks and throw the rest as you see fit.

##### Transformative Familiar (Ex) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

Your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) is able to assume the shape of a single masterwork amulet, ring, staff, [*<u>wand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands), or weapon. While it’s in this form, treat the [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) as an intelligent magic item with the [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar)‘s mental ability scores but no magical abilities. You can add magical enhancements, intelligent item powers, and intelligent item senses as if you possessed the appropriate crafting feat. (For example, if your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) transforms into a staff, you could craft it into an intelligent staff of fire as if you possessed the [<u>Craft Staff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/item-creation-feats/craft-staff-item-creation) feat.) You must still pay for enhancements as normal.

As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), the [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) can transform into and out of its item form and does so in such a way that you can have it immediately equipped when the transformation is complete. While in item form, your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) retains its original [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), but gains the [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) of the item it transforms into. If your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) takes enough points of damage in item form to become [<u>broken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Broken), it automatically reverts to its [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) form and cannot change to item form again until it is healed to its maximum hit point total. You must have a [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard#TOC-Arcane-Bond-Ex-or-Sp-) to select this path ability.

#### 3rd-Tier Archmage Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Arcane Metamastery (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power and pick any one [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) you know that increases the slot level of the spell by 0 or 1 levels. For the next 10 rounds, you can apply this [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) to any arcane spell you cast without increasing the spell slot used or [<u>casting time</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Casting-Time).

You can also use this ability on an arcane spell you cast from a [*<u>scroll</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/scrolls), staff, or [*<u>wand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands). You can’t have more than one use of this ability active at a time. If you use this ability again, any previous use immediately ends (though this doesn’t affect spells already cast). You can select this ability more than once. Each time you select this ability, the maximum spell slot level increase of the [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) you can choose goes up by 1, to a maximum of 4.

##### Arcane Potency (Su) \[2\]

Choose one of your arcane spellcasting classes. You gain four bonus 1st-level arcane spell slots for that class (like the bonus spells gained from a high ability score). This ability doesn’t change your number of spells known. You can select this ability more than once. If you choose the same arcane spellcasting class a second time, you gain four bonus 2nd-level spell slots; if you choose it again, you gain 3rdlevel slots for choosing it a third time, and so on. This ability can’t grant you bonus spells for a spell level that you don’t have the ability to cast.

##### Bloodline Immunity (Su) \[1\]

The intense power of your [<u>sorcerer</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer) blood makes you immune to the weapons you rely on. You don’t take damage or suffer harmful effects from spells on your [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) spell list, even ones you aren’t of a high enough level to cast. If you’re targeted by any of these spells, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to turn the spell back upon the original caster as if using [*<u>spell turning</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/spell-turning).

Beneficial effects of spells on your [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) spell list affect you normally. You must have the [<u>bloodline</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/sorcerer#TOC-Bloodline) class feature to select this ability.

##### Component Freedom (Ex) \[1\]

When you cast an arcane spell, you can ignore one component of the spell: focus, material, somatic, or verbal. You can ignore a focus or material component only if its total value is equal to or less than 100 gp × your tier. You can select this ability more than once; each time you take this ability, the number of [<u>components</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Components) you may ignore for each spell you cast increases by 1.

##### Eldritch Flight (Sp, Su) \[1\]

You gain a bonus on [<u>Fly</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/fly) checks equal to your tier. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to fly (as the fly spell) for a number of minutes equal to your tier. At 6th tier, you instead gain a supernatural fly speed of 60 feet (40 feet if you wear medium or heavy [<u>armor</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus), or are carrying a medium or heavy load).

##### Infectious Spell (Su) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You know no limits when it comes to the potency and ravaging effects of your spells. Whenever you cast a spell that specifically targets a limited number of targets (such as “one creature/level” or “one or more creatures”) and has a duration longer than 1 round, you can expend one use of mythic power to make the spell infectious. Each round on your turn, so long as at least one creature is still under the effect of the infectious spell, the spell targets one additional enemy per mythic tier you possess. The range of the spell is the same, but it originates from a creature currently affected by the spell (your choice). A creature affected by an infectious spell suffers all of the effects of the original spell (and may attempt any saving throws as appropriate at the original save DC), but the duration does not reset (meaning the effect ends for all affected targets when the original duration of the spell expires).

A creature can only be targeted by a given infectious spell once per casting. Once a round passes without any additional creatures affected by the spell, the spell stops being infectious. This ability can only affect spells whose level is equal to or less than double your tier.

##### Many Forms (Su) \[2\]

You can alter your appearance at will as if using [*<u>alter self</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/alter-self). You can expend one use of mythic power to [<u>change shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Change-Shape-Su-) as if using [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph), with a duration of 1 minute per tier. Your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) for this ability is a number equal to 10 + your tier. At 6th tier, the duration of the [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) ability increases to 10 minutes per tier.

##### Mirror Dodge (Su) \[2\]

When hit by a melee or ranged attack, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to replace yourself with an illusory duplicate and teleport to any open square within 30 feet of your current position. You take no damage from this attack, which instead destroys your illusory duplicate (similar to [*<u>mirror image</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mirror-image)). Using this ability requires a line of effect to the square into which you teleport.

##### Mythic Spellpower (Sp) \[1\]

You have a pool of magical power you can draw upon for casting mythic spells. Up to twice per day, you can use this power to cast a mythic spell without expending any uses of mythic power. You can select this ability up to three times; each time you do, you gain two additional uses of this ability per day.

##### Reverse Scrying (Su) \[1\]

When you notice a [*<u>scrying</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/scrying) sensor created by a [*<u>scrying</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/scrying) magical effect, you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) to use that sensor to observe its creator. If the initial [*<u>scrying</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/scrying) effect allows the target a saving throw, the sensor’s creator can attempt a saving throw with a DC equal to 10 plus double your tier to realize you are attempting to observe it and block this attempt. If you act strangely once you notice the sensor, its creator might realize you’re aware of it; at the GM’s discretion, you may need to attempt a [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff) check against the creator’s [<u>Sense Motive</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sense-motive). If your reverse [*<u>scrying</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/scrying) goes unnoticed, you can expend an additional use of mythic power to control what the caster perceives, as if you had cast [*<u>false vision</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/false-vision) with a duration of [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) and a [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) equal to double your tier.

##### Speedy Summons (Su) \[69\]

When you cast a [*<u>summon monster</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-monster) spell, the [<u>casting time</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Casting-Time) is 1 [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) instead of 1 round. If you expend one use of mythic power, the [<u>casting time</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Casting-Time) becomes 1 [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions). If you’re a [<u>summoner</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner), as a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) your [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon).

##### Spell Sieve (Sp) \[1\]

If you successfully [<u>counterspell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) an arcane spell that is on your spell list and of a level you can cast, you can absorb the countered spell’s power and use it for your own purposes. On your next turn, you can cast that spell as a [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-) using your arcane spellcaster level as the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level).

##### Tangible Illusion (Su) \[1\]

You can temporarily transform an illusory object into a real one. As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to temporarily transform a single object created by an [<u>illusion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Illusion) spell that you cast into a physical, non-magical version of that object. The object lasts for 10 minutes per tier, after which it reverts to being an [<u>illusion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Illusion) (or vanishes, if the duration of the original [<u>illusion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Illusion) has expired). During that time, the illusion gains all the physical properties of the depicted illusory object. You must touch the illusion to use this ability, and the object can be no larger than 5 cubic feet per tier. For example, if you create an illusory stone door, you transform it into an actual non-magical stone door.

##### Teleportation Master (Su) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You have become an expert at [<u>teleportation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Teleportation) magic. You can expend one use of mythic power when casting any spell of the [<u>teleportation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Teleportation) subschool to visualize your destination before you fully materialize there, gaining sensory information of the area as though via a [*<u>clairaudience/clairvoyance</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/clairaudience-clairvoyance) spell. After gaining this sensory information, you can choose to alter your [<u>teleportation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Teleportation) destination by up to 100 feet in any direction. The new destination must be a legal location for the effect.

#### 6th-Tier Archmage Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Channel Power (Su)

You gain the ability to channel raw arcane power into a spell. You can also expend one use of mythic power when casting an arcane spell to increase its damage by 50%. If the spell has a duration greater than 1 round, the duration doubles. Any saves required by the spell take a –4 penalty, although for mythic creatures, this penalty is reduced to –2. This spell ignores any [<u>spell resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Spell-Resistance) the targets have, although targets immune to the spell or to magic still retain that protection.

##### Divine [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) (Ex)

You can use arcane power to cast a small number of divine spells. Choose three 1stlevel spells from the [<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) spell list or three 1st-level spells from the [<u>druid</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid) spell list. You can add those spells to your spellbook (if you’re a [<u>magus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/magus) or [<u>wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard)) or [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) (if you’re a [<u>witch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/witch)) for free and can cast those spells as if they were arcane spells on your class list. You can select this ability up to three times. Each time you select it, you choose three more spells from the same class’s spell list ([<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) or [<u>druid</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid)), and the highest spell level you can select from increases by 1 (up to 2nd level the second time and up to 3rd level the third time). You must be a [<u>magus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/magus), [<u>witch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/witch), or [<u>wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/wizard) to select this ability.

##### Dominion over Outsiders (Ex)

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You have a way with words when dealing with [<u>extraplanar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Extraplanar) forces called to the [<u>Material Plane</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/environment/the-planes#TOC-Material-Plane1) to serve you. When bargaining with [<u>outsiders</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) called via a [*<u>planar binding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-binding) or similar spell, you add a bonus equal to your tier on [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) checks against the called creature and on [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) checks to prevent the creature from escaping your magic circle. In addition, you can expend a number of uses of mythic power equal to half the [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-) of the [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) to attempt to bind the [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) to perform a task for up to a year and a day. The creature gains a +5 bonus on the opposed [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) check to refuse this request. If the [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) agrees to carry out this request, it is bound to perform that task for up to a year and a day or until otherwise released (either by completing the task or by your will). You cannot regain that use of mythic power until the [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) is released, effectively decreasing your total number of uses of mythic power per day by the number of uses spent to bind the [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider).

##### Eldritch Reciprocation (Su)

You can choose to take extra damage from an arcane spell to replenish mythic power. When you are hit by or fail a saving throw against an arcane spell that deals hit point damage, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can choose to take damage as if that spell were affected by the [<u>Maximize Spell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats/maximize-spell-metamagic) feat. If the spell’s damage can’t be increased by the [<u>Maximize Spell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats/maximize-spell-metamagic) feat or is already under the effect of the [<u>Maximize Spell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats/maximize-spell-metamagic) feat, you instead take double damage from it. The spell’s damage ignores your [<u>spell resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Spell-Resistance), [<u>energy resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Energy-Resistance), energy immunities, and other protections against damage.

If you take at least 25 points of damage from the spell, you regain one use of mythic power.

##### Sanctum (Su)

You gain access to your own personal [<u>extradimensional</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Extradimensional-Spaces) sanctum. By concentrating for 1 minute without interruption, you conjure the door to your own personal sanctum, similar to [*<u>mage’s magnificent mansion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mage-s-magnificent-mansion), but with the following differences. The sanctum takes up a space equal to six 20-foot cubes per tier, shapeable when you first create it as if it were a spell whose area or effect is shapeable. The sanctum is permanent and persistent, so you can store objects within the sanctum and retrieve them on future visits. The sanctum includes one [*<u>unseen servant</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/u/unseen-servant) per tier. When you conjure the sanctum’s door, you decide which creatures can see and use it (to all other creatures it’s [<u>invisible</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Invisible) and impermeable). The door remains until you dismiss it—a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) with unlimited range as long as you are on the same plane as the door. If you dismiss the door while you are outside the sanctum, all creatures other than your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) are immediately shunted outside. Conjuring the door automatically dismisses any other active door. As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), your [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar) can enter or leave the sanctum from any square adjacent to you, without using the conjured door.

##### Star Walker (Su)

**Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You can transform your power to enable you to travel great distances. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend two uses of mythic power to surround yourself with a bubble of air enclosed by a thin iridescent layer of force. While this bubble offers no protection from attacks, you can propel yourself through the air at great speed. As long as you concentrate, you gain a fly speed of 240 feet per round.
