If you cease concentrating, the bubble stops and then sinks 30 feet per round until it reaches the ground and collapses; you can expend one use of mythic power before the bubble collapses to regain control.

In the void of outer space, the bubble’s speed is much faster. Although exact travel times vary, a trip within a single solar system takes 3d20 hours, while a trip beyond takes 3d20 days (or more, at the GM’s discretion). Ceasing [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) while in the void of outer space has no effect on the bubble—it continues traveling in the previously stated direction at the same speed until an outside force slows it down. The air in the sphere is constantly refreshed and kept at a constant temperature, protecting occupants from the void of space, but not necessarily the harsh climates of other planets or planes. By expending two additional uses of mythic power, you can increase the size of the bubble to a 10-foot-radius sphere and you can bring along up to 11 Medium creatures within the bubble.

Other creatures can enter or exit the bubble freely.

#### 1st-Tier Champion Path Abilities

##### Distant Barrage (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to make a ranged attack at your highest [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). This is in addition to any other attacks you make this round. When making this attack, ignore the target’s [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover) and [<u>concealment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Concealment) other than total [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover), and add your tier to the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll). Damage from this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Fleet Charge (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to move up to your speed. At any point during this movement, you can make a single melee or ranged attack at your highest [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus), adding your tier to the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll). This is in addition to any other attacks you make this round. Damage from this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Sudden Attack (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to make a melee attack at your highest [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). This is in addition to any other attacks you make this round. When making a sudden attack, you roll twice and take the better result, adding your tier to the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll). Damage from this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Aerial Assault (Su) \[1\]

> You can [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) at creatures in the air, or leap across obstacles as part of a charge. When making a charge attack, you can expend one use of mythic power to include a single [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) check made to jump, adding 10 feet per tier to the height or distance you jump. You take no falling damage from the height gained as part of this leap. If your attack hits, you may deal an amount of additional damage equal to the falling damage appropriate for the height you reached. Alternatively, you may replace your melee attack from this [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) with a [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) check. If you successfully [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) a creature, you bring it to the ground with you at the end of your jump, and it takes an appropriate amount of falling damage for the height it was at when you [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled) it.

##### Always a Chance (Ex) \[1\]

> You don’t automatically miss when you roll a 1 on an [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll).

##### Always Armed (Ex) \[1\]

> You don’t take any penalties from using an improvised weapon. In addition, whenever you confirm a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) with an improvised weapon, you can treat its critical multiplier as ×3 instead of ×2, but the weapon is destroyed.

##### Armor Master (Ex) \[1\]

> You don’t take an armor check penalty or incur a arcane spell failure chance when wearing light armor or using a shield (including a tower shield). In addition, the maximum [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) bonus of light armor doesn’t apply to you. You can select this ability up to three times. The second time, it also applies to medium armor. The third time, it also applies to heavy armor.

##### Backlash (Ex) \[1\]

> You strike back at those who penetrate your defenses. Whenever an adjacent enemy confirms a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against you, that enemy also provokes an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from you. If you have used all of your [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) for the round, you may expend one use of mythic power to make this [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) anyway.

##### Blowback (Ex) \[1\]

> As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to make one melee or ranged attack at your full [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). If the attack hits, your foe is also knocked directly away from you a distance equal to 20 feet per tier. If the foe strikes a solid object before reaching this distance, it takes 1d6 points of bludgeoning damage for every 10 feet it couldn’t travel.
>
> If it strikes another creature, both it and the creature it strikes take half this damage.

##### Burst Through (Ex) \[1\]

> When you [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge), you can move through allies and opponents almost as if they were not there to obstruct your path. You can move through squares containing allies freely, but you must succeed at an [<u>overrun</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Overrun) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check for each opponent that obstructs your path to the target of your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge).
>
> If the result of the check exceeds the opponent’s [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense), you may move through the opponent’s square and continue toward the target without provoking an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from that opponent. If you fail any of these [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) checks, your movement ends in the square before that opponent, but you may resolve the [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) attack against the foe that stopped you.

##### Clean Blade (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can make a ranged [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) to fling the blood and gore at another opponent within 30 feet. If the [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) hits, the foe is [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened) for a number of rounds equal to your tier. If the [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) is a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), the foe is also [<u>blinded</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Blinded) for the same duration. A [<u>blinded</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Blinded) foe can spend a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions) to remove the gore and end the blindness.
>
> At the GM’s discretion, creatures that are immune to [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases), those that live in filth (such as [<u>otyughs</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/aberrations/otyugh)), or those that revel in bloodshed (such as [<u>demons</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/outsiders/demon)) might be immune to the [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened) effect of this ability.

##### Climbing Master (Ex) \[1\]

> You gain a climb speed equal to your base land speed and a +8 [<u>racial bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Racial-Bonus) on [<u>Climb</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/climb) checks. If you already have a climb speed, your base climb speed increases by 30 feet. In addition, you retain your [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) bonus to AC while climbing. You can climb perfectly flat or smooth surfaces, treating them as if they had a [<u>Climb</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/climb) DC of 40.

##### Crusader (Ex)

> Your prowess and ability draw countless followers to your banner. You gain followers as if you had the [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) feat. In addition, you add your tier to your leadership score when determining the number of followers you gain. Whenever you are within 100 feet of such followers, each follower can use the surge ability once per day without needing to expend mythic power.
>
> The followers use the same die type as your surge ability.
>
> If you have or gain the [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) feat, you gain followers from both this ability and the [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) feat (in effect doubling the number of followers gained).

##### Devastating Smash (Su) \[1\]

> Whenever you attack a [<u>construct</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct) or object, treat its [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) (or [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) in the case of [<u>constructs</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct)) as 10 less than normal. Add your tier to damage rolls against [<u>constructs</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct) and objects. If you expend one use of mythic power as part of an attack against a [<u>construct</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct) or object, you treat its [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) (or [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction)) as 0 and add double your tier to damage instead.

##### Endless Hatred (Ex) \[1\]

> You can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) to increase all of your [<u>favored enemy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger#TOC-Favored-Enemy-Ex-) bonuses by 2 for 1 minute. When you use this ability, for the rest of your turn any attacks you make against a [<u>favored enemy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger#TOC-Favored-Enemy-Ex-) bypass all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). You must have the [<u>favored enemy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger#TOC-Favored-Enemy-Ex-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Ever Ready (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you make an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), you gain a bonus on the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) and damage roll equal to your mythic tier. You can make [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) while [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed), even if you don’t have the [<u>Combat Reflexes</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/combat-reflexes-combat) feat. At 3rd, 6th, and 9th tier, the number of [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) you can make each round increases by one.

##### Flash of Rage (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you or an ally within 30 feet is struck by a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), you can enter a [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) on your next turn. This [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) functions as the [<u>barbarian</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian) class feature, and lasts a number of rounds equal to your tier. You aren’t [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued) after this [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-). If you have the [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) class feature, these rounds don’t count toward your daily uses of that ability, you can use abilities that require patience and [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) while in this [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) (although you are still restricted when using your [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) class feature normally), and these rounds of [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) don’t count toward the total number of rounds of [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) used when determining how long you are [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued). Rounds of [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) granted by this ability don’t stack, but the duration is reset if another [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) occurs.

##### Impossible Speed (Ex) \[1\]

> Your base land speed increases by 30 feet. In addition, if you expend one use of mythic power, for 1 hour your base land speed increases by 10 feet per mythic tier.

##### Imprinting Hand (Su) \[1\]

> By touching a foe, you can gain knowledge about it, including its weaknesses. To use this ability, you must first successfully hit a foe with an [<u>unarmed strike</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/strike-unarmed), [<u>natural weapon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks), or melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) to make contact, then use this ability is a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions). As long as the target remains within 1 mile of you, you always know the direction and approximate distance to it. If the foe has any weaknesses or [<u>vulnerabilities</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Vulnerabilities-Ex-or-Su-) (including a mythic flaw), you immediately know this information.
>
> You can maintain this connection with only one creature at a time; if you use this ability on another creature, your connection with the previous creature is lost.

##### Juggernaut (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Little can stand in your way when you [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) forth into battle—even solid doors and walls don’t stand a chance. While charging, you may attempt a free [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) check to break through a single door or wall in your path. You can expend one use of mythic power while performing this [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) to gain a +10 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) check to break the object. If you destroy the object, you can continue your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) as though it were never there. If you fail to destroy the object, your movement ends in the square before that object. Note that destroying integral parts of a structure may trigger a collapse (treat as a cave-in) at the GM’s discretion. This ability can be combined with other path abilities and effects that modify [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) attacks, such as the burst through path ability. You can select this ability up to three times. Each additional time you select it, you may break through one more wall or door during your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge), and the [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus) on your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) check increases by 10 (to a maximum of a +30 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus)).

##### Lesson Learned (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you fail a saving throw against a supernatural or [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-), you gain a +5 bonus on all further saving throws against that ability, including subsequent saves against an ongoing effect, or against the same effect produced by a different source or creature. This bonus lasts for a number of minutes equal to your tier. For example, if you fail a save against a [<u>demon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Demon) [*<u>fireball</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/fireball) spell-like ability, you gain this bonus against a [*<u>fireball</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/fireball) spell-like ability from any creature; if you fail a save against a [<u>blue dragon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/dragons/dragon/chromatic-blue) electricity [<u>breath weapon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Breath-Weapon-Su-), you gain this bonus against all [<u>blue dragon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/dragons/dragon/chromatic-blue) electricity breath weapons, but not against a [<u>black dragon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/dragons/dragon/chromatic-black) acid breath weapon or a [<u>gorgon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listin/magical-beasts/gorgon) petrification breath weapon. If you fail another saving throw against the same ability, the duration of lesson learned resets but the bonus doesn’t stack with itself.

##### Limitless Range (Ex) \[1\]

> Multiply the range increment on all of your ranged and thrown weapons by 5 feet, and these weapons no longer have a maximum range increment for you. You can throw any melee weapon as if it had a range increment of 20 feet—this increment isn’t multiplied by 5, but the weapon doesn’t have a maximum range increment.

##### Meat Shield (Ex) \[1\]

> You can use a creature you’re [<u>grappling</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) to protect you from harm. If you’re [<u>grappling</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) a creature and are attacked with a melee or ranged attack, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can attempt a [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against your [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled) opponent. If you succeed, the incoming attack targets your [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled) opponent instead of you. If you fail, the grappled opponent breaks free from the grapple and the incoming attack targets you as normal.

##### Mounted Maniac (Ex) \[2\]

> Your unstoppable momentum while mounted is terrifying. Whenever you [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) a creature while mounted, you can attempt an [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check to demoralize all enemies within 30 feet of your target, adding your tier to the result of the check. If you expend one use of mythic power, any creature demoralized by this ability is [<u>frightened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Frightened) instead of [<u>shaken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Shaken). The duration of this condition is dependent upon your check, as noted in the [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) skill.

##### Mule’s Strength (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You treat your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) score as 5 higher when determining your carrying capacity. This bonus applies to encumbrance, the maximum weight you can lift over your head, dragging and pushing, and so on.
>
> You can take this path ability multiple times. Each time you do, you treat your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) score as an additional 5 points higher when determining your carrying capacity.

##### Mythic Ki (Su) \[1\]

> As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to regain 2 points in your [*<u>ki</u>* <u>pool</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/monk#TOC-Ki-Pool-Su-) (up to your maximum). For the rest of your turn, your successful attacks with unarmed strikes or [<u>monk</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/monk) weapons bypass all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). You must have the [*<u>ki</u>* <u>pool</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/monk#TOC-Ki-Pool-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Mythic Rage (Su) \[1\]

> As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to regain 1/4 your maximum number of [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) rounds (minimum 4 rounds). For the rest of your turn, your successful attacks while [<u>raging</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) bypass all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). You must have the [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Mythic Smite (Su) \[1\]

> As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to regain one use of your [<u>smite evil</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) ability. For the rest of your turn, your successful attacks against evil creatures bypass all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). You must have the [<u>smite evil</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Mythic Weapon Training (Ex) \[1\]

> Select one group of weapons from the list of [<u>fighter</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/fighter) weapon groups. You gain proficiency with all weapons in this group. If you possess a feat such as [<u>Weapon Focus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/weapon-focus-combat) that requires you to choose a kind of weapon, you can instead apply the effects of that feat to all weapons from that weapon group. When wielding a weapon from that group, add a number equal to your tier to your [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense) against [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm) and [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) attempts made against that weapon. You can select this ability more than once. Each time you select this ability, it applies to a different weapon group.

##### Punishing Blow (Ex) \[1\]

> Any opponent you hit with a melee or ranged attack loses the benefits of [<u>regeneration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Regeneration-Ex-) and [<u>fast healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules/#Fast_Healing_Ex) for 1 round. In addition, if you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against the target, it loses the benefit of its [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) for 1 round. A creature whose [<u>regeneration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Regeneration-Ex-) can’t be suppressed or ignored (such as the [<u>tarrasque</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/magical-beasts/tarrasque)) is immune to this effect.

##### Sniper’s Riposte (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You’re an expert at defending yourself while aiming a ranged weapon. You don’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from making ranged attacks. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to negate the [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) check penalty for sniping for 1 round.

##### Sunder Storm (Ex) \[1\]

> As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) against each opponent within reach, selecting one item on each opponent to [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder). The damage from these [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) attempts ignores item [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness). If you destroy an item in this way, you drive the shattered pieces of the item into the flesh of its wearer or wielder, dealing 1d6 + a number of points equal to your tier of piercing damage. This damage bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Swimming Master (Ex) \[1\]

> You gain a swim speed equal to your base land speed and a +8 [<u>racial bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Racial-Bonus) on [<u>Swim</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/swim) checks. If you already have a swim speed, your base swim speed increases by 30 feet. You can hold your breath for a number of minutes equal to 10 × your [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) score, but afterward you must succeed at checks as normal to avoid drowning. If you expend one use of mythic power, for 1 hour your base swim speed increases by 10 feet per mythic tier.

##### Tear Apart (Ex) \[1\]

> You can attempt to [<u>rend</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rend-Ex-) the armor of your opponent, reducing its effectiveness. Using this ability is a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) that requires you to attempt a [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against your foe. If the check is successful, reduce the target’s [<u>armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus), [<u>natural armor</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Natural-Armor-Bonus) bonus, or [<u>shield bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Shield-Bonus) (your choice) by your tier x2 (minimum 2). If the creature has an [<u>enhancement bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Enhancement-Bonus) to the bonus you chose, reduce the normal bonus first, then apply any leftover reduction to the [<u>enhancement bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Enhancement-Bonus). You can’t reduce the bonus below 0. Armor and shields damaged in this way can be repaired as if they had the [<u>broken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Broken) condition. Natural armor regains the lost bonus at the rate of 1 point per day. [*<u>Lesser restoration</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/restoration) restores 1d4 points of [<u>natural armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Natural-Armor-Bonus), and [*<u>restoration</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/restoration) restores all of it.

##### Titan’s Bane (Ex) \[1\]

> You can move through the space of any creature two or more size categories larger than you without provoking [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), and you can share such a creature’s space. When sharing a larger opponent’s space, you gain [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover) against all melee and ranged attacks made by the creature, and it is considered [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed) for the purposes of any melee or ranged attacks you make against it.

##### Uncanny Grapple (Ex) \[1\]

> Upon making a successful [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against a creature you are [<u>grappling</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple), you can perform one of the following actions: throw, crush, or swing. For a throw, you can throw the target of your [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) up to 20 feet per tier; if the creature strikes a solid object before reaching this distance, it takes 1d6 points of damage per 10 feet of the remaining throwing distance and lands [<u>prone</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Prone). For a crush, you deal 1d6 points of non-lethal damage per tier.
>
> For a swing, you can use the target as a weapon against another creature, treating the [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled) creature as a twohanded weapon. If you succeed at a melee attack against an opponent adjacent to the target, both that opponent and the grappled creature take 1d8 points of bludgeoning damage from this attack + 1-1/2 × your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) modifier.

##### Wall Smasher (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you succeed at a ki throw or a [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) or [<u>reposition</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reposition) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers), you can smash your opponent against a wall (or suitable large obstacle) within the range you could move the target into as a result of the maneuver, dealing [<u>unarmed strike</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/strike-unarmed) damage to your opponent and the wall. If you expend one use of mythic power when you use this ability, this damage ignores the [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) of the wall. If the damage destroys the wall, you move the opponent into the space beyond the wall and it immediately falls [<u>prone</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Prone).

#### 3rd-Tier Champion Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Destroyer (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you attack an object, including a held or worn item, you ignore any [<u>Hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) the object might possess. This includes spell effects such as [*<u>wall of force</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/w/wall-of-force), and also creatures, such as [<u>animated objects</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/constructs/animated-object).

##### Disabling Strike (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> With your mighty strikes, you can brutally batter the limbs of your foes. Whenever you score a successful [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against an adjacent creature, as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to potentially disable one of that creature’s limbs. Mythic creatures receive a [<u>Fortitude</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Fortitude) save to negate this effect (DC = 10 + your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) modifier + your tier), while non-mythic creatures do not. An affected creature takes 1d4 points of [<u>Constitution damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Ability-Score-Damage) and one of its limbs (your choice) is mangled. If you choose to disable an arm, the creature drops anything held in that arm, cannot hold items in that arm, and makes [<u>natural attacks</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks) with that arm at a –4 penalty. If you choose to disable a leg, the target’s base movement speed is halved. These penalties are removed when the [<u>Constitution damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Ability-Score-Damage) taken from this ability is healed. Foes that are immune to critical hits are immune to this ability, as are those with unusual anatomies (subject to GM discretion).

##### Elemental Fury (Su) \[1\]

> You can cause your body to erupt with elemental power. As a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power and pick one of the following energy types: acid, cold, electricity, or fire. For a number of rounds equal to your tier, you are immune to this energy type and all of your melee and ranged attacks deal an extra 1d6 points of damage of this energy type. On a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), this extra energy damage increases to 3d6.

##### Fleet Warrior (Ex) \[1\]

> When making a full attack, you can move up to your speed either before or after your attacks. This movement provokes [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) as normal.

##### Groundshaker (Su) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> As a Move [<u>action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to stomp or punch the ground with mighty force, forcing creatures within 10 feet per tier to attempt a [<u>Reflex</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reflex) save (DC = 10 + your tier + your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) modifier).
>
> Non-mythic creatures that fail their saves fall [<u>prone</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Prone) and are [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered) for 1 round. Non-mythic creatures that succeed at their saves and mythic creatures that fail are [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered) for 1 round. Mythic creatures that succeed at their saves are unaffected. You are not affected by this ability, and you may designate a number of creatures up to your tier to also not be affected.

##### Incredible Parry (Ex) \[1\]

> You gain the [<u>duelist’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/prestige-classes/core-rulebook/duelist) parry ability, and may expend one use of mythic power to add your tier to the parry roll. If you already have the duelist’s parry ability, you can use incredible parry and duelist’s parry in the same round, electing to not make two attacks and instead parrying up to two attacks.

##### Maneuver Expert (Ex) \[1\]

> You do not provoke any [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) when you attempt a [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check.
>
> As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of your mythic power whenever you attempt a [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check to gain the benefits of the Improved and Greater feats tied to that maneuver. You must decide to use this ability before making the roll.

##### Master Grappler (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You are a legendary wrestler, and few can withstand your mighty throws, holds, and blows. While using the uncanny [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) path ability, if you throw a creature into a solid object, that object takes the same amount of damage as the creature; if this is enough to break the object, the creature continues to move through it. If you throw the creature into another creature, resolve the attack as a [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) by you, except the thrown creature moves with the [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) and does not incur any [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) for moving through [<u>threatened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Threatened-Squares) squares. If you crush the creature, you can choose to deal either 1d6 points of lethal damage per tier or 1d8 points of [<u>nonlethal damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Nonlethal-Damage) per tier, and you can limit the damage you deal to the amount required to render the target [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious). If you swing the creature as a weapon, add your tier to the amount of damage you deal to both creatures; if you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), the target of the swing attack also takes damage from a melee [<u>natural attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks) from the creature you are swinging (if it has one; choose randomly if it has more than one). You must have the uncanny [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) champion path ability to take this ability.

##### Maximized Critical (Ex) \[2\]

> Whenever you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), the weapon’s damage result is always the maximum possible amount you could roll. This does affect other dice added to the damage, such as from [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) or the [*<u>flaming</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/magic-weapons/magic-weapon-special-abilities/flaming) weapon special ability. For example, if you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) with a [<u>longsword</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/longsword) (1d8/×2), treat the sword’s damage dice as if you had rolled 8 both times, then add any other damage bonuses that you would normally apply to a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits).

##### Mighty Hurler (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You gain the [<u>rock throwing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rock-Throwing-Ex-) and [<u>rock catching</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rock-Catching-Ex-) universal monster abilities. The range increment of your [<u>rock throwing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rock-Throwing-Ex-) ability is 10 feet per tier. You can use these abilities with any solid, mostly inflexible object, not just rocks. The maximum size of an object you can throw is two size categories smaller than you. You can take this ability three times. Each additional time you select it, the range increment increases by an additional 10 feet per tier and the maximum size of an object you can throw increases by one size category.

##### Penetrating Damage (Su) \[1\]

> Whenever you deal damage to a creature with [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction), you can overcome one of the following types of [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction): [<u>chaotic</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Chaotic), [<u>cold iron</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/special-materials#TOC-Iron-Cold), evil, good, [<u>lawful</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Lawful), magic, or silver. You can change this type with each attack. This ability only aids in overcoming one type of [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction); if the creature’s [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) requires a combination of different types of damage, you must be able to overcome the other types using other means.

##### Precision (Ex) \[1\]

> Your deadly attacks are far more likely to hit their target than those of others. Whenever you make a full attack, your [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) on the additional attacks you gain by having a high [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) is 5 higher. This ability can’t give any of these attacks a higher [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) than your [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). For example, a 12th-level [<u>fighter</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/fighter) normally has a [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) of +12/+7/+2; with this ability, his [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) is +12/+12/+7. This ability doesn’t reduce the penalties from two-weapon fighting or other situational penalties on [<u>attack rolls</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) (such as [<u>Combat Expertise</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/combat-expertise-combat), [<u>Power Attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/power-attack-combat), fighting defensively, or harmful conditions). You can select this ability more than once. Each time you select it, the [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) on additional attacks increases by another 5.

##### Titan’s Rage (Su) \[1\]

> You can cause yourself to grow in [<u>size</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Size-Bonus), becoming a terrifying sight to behold. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) (or a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) if you have the [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) class feature), you can expend one use of mythic power to increase your [<u>size</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Size-Bonus) to one size category larger. This grants you a +4 [<u>size bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Size-Bonus) to [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) and [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-), imposes a –2 penalty to [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-), grants you a +2 [<u>natural armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Natural-Armor-Bonus), increases your natural reach by 5 feet, and increases your weapon damage based on the new size of your weapons.
>
> This [<u>size</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Size-Bonus) increase lasts a number of rounds equal to your tier, and doesn’t stack with any other spell or ability that alters your [<u>size</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Size-Bonus).

##### To the Death (Ex) \[1\]

> You can shrug off wounds that others would find devastating. When below 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), you don’t fall [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious), but are instead [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered). You lose 1 hit point at the end of each turn when you take a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) while [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered) in this way.

##### Unstoppable Shot (Ex) \[1\]

> You can use a ranged or thrown weapon to make a single powerful shot that shoots through all creatures in a line. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), make a single ranged [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) and resolve the attack against all targets in a straight line up to the maximum range of your weapon, stopping at any barrier the attack couldn’t penetrate. Roll damage once and apply that damage to each creature hit by the attack. Before making the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll), you can expend one use of mythic power to bend the path of the attack up to two times as it strikes targets or objects. Each [<u>deflection</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Bonus-Deflection-) can alter the line of the attack up to 90 degrees, allowing you to shoot around corners or [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover).

#### 6th-Tier Champion Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Critical Master (Ex)

> Whenever you roll a critical threat against a non-mythic creature, you automatically confirm the [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) and deal the maximum amount of damage to that creature. This ability can be selected twice. The second time it is selected, it also applies to mythic creatures.

##### Fistful of Daggers (Ex)

> When you could make a full attack with thrown weapons, you can instead make a single [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) at your highest [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) and apply the result to any number of creatures in a 30-foot cone.
>
> Roll damage once and deal it to all creatures hit by the attack. This attack can’t deal precision damage. You must throw one weapon at each target. Use the bonuses to attack and damage from the least powerful weapon thrown (for example, if you have one +2 [<u>dagger</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/dagger) and 20 masterwork daggers, the attacks are treated as masterwork daggers).
>
> Apply any effects of [<u>concealment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Concealment) for individual targets as normal. If the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) is a critical threat, choose one target you hit and attempt to confirm the critical against that target.

##### Perfect Strike (Ex)

You can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) to deliver a perfect strike. A perfect strike attack is made using your full [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). If the attack hits, it deals double the normal amount of damage, and this damage bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) and [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness). If the perfect strike is a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), increase the critical multiplier for the attack by 1 (so a ×2 weapon deals ×3 damage). Damage that isn’t multiplied on a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) isn’t multiplied on a perfect strike. Perfect strike can be used in conjunction with any champion’s strike if you expend uses of mythic power for each ability separately.

##### Seven-League Leap (Ex)

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

Your leap is so mighty that you defy gravity. Add your tier to [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) checks made to jump. The distance you can jump in a round is not limited by your movement speed. If you are carrying no more than a light load, you can expend one use of mythic power to make a powerful jump that lets you sail through the air. In order to use this ability, you must be able to run in a straight line for 1 minute. Any obstacles or impediments that prevent you from completing this sprint uninterrupted prevent you from being able to use this ability, though the expenditure of mythic power is not wasted. At the end of your 1-minute sprint, you attempt an [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) check and leap a distance up to half the check’s result in miles, rounded down to the nearest mile (for example, an [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) check result of 29 would allow you to jump 14 miles). This trip takes 1 round per mile, and you reach a maximum height at the apex of your arc equal to half the distance traveled. You do not take falling damage from using this ability. You must have a clear arc of travel to complete this jump; if you strike an obstacle mid-jump, you and the obstacle each take a number of points of damage equal to 1d8 × the number of miles you have left to travel. If this damage destroys the obstacle, you continue your jump; otherwise, your jump comes to an end and you fall, taking falling damage as appropriate. You cannot aim this leap accurately, and always land 50 to 5,000 feet (5d%) from your intended destination.

##### Shatter Spells (Su)

You can destroy a magical effect (whether it’s on a creature or an independent effect such as a [*<u>wall of fire</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/w/wall-of-fire)) by attacking it with an [<u>unarmed strike</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/strike-unarmed) or [<u>natural weapon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks). You must succeed at a melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) against the creature or effect and expend one use of mythic power. If this attack hits, the creature or effect is subject to targeted greater [*<u>dispel magic</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dispel-magic), using double your tier as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level). If you dispel an effect, you suffer no harmful effects from touching it. If the effect is on a creature, the creature takes 1 point of damage per spell level of each effect dispelled.

##### Sweeping Strike (Su)

Even when you’re swarmed, your foes are not safe from danger. Whenever you could make a full attack, you may instead make a single attack at your highest [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) and apply the results to all opponents within your reach. Roll damage once and apply it to all opponents hit by the attack. This attack can’t deal precision damage. If the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) is a critical threat, choose one target you hit and attempt to confirm the critical against that target.

#### 1st-Tier Guardian Path Abilities

You can select these path abilities at any tier.

##### Absorb Blow (Su) \[1\]

As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), whenever you take hit point damage from a single source (such as a dragon’s breath, a spell, or a weapon), you can expend one use of mythic power to reduce the damage you take from that source by 15 per tier (to a minimum of 0 points of damage taken). If you have another ability or effect that reduces damage (such as [*<u>protection from energy</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/protection-from-energy)), reduce the damage with the absorb blow ability before applying any other damage-reducing effects. For every 10 points of damage that this ability prevents, for 1 minute you gain [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 3/epic and 5 points of resistance against acid, cold, electricity, fire, and sonic damage. The [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) and resistances stack with any other [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) and resistances that you have.

##### Beast’s Fury (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to imbue your [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>cohort</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded [*<u>mount</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mount) with some of your mythic power. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), that creature can move up to its speed and make an attack with one of its [<u>natural weapons</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks). When making this attack, the creature rolls twice and takes the higher result. Any damage dealt by this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). A creature affected by this ability can take these actions in addition to any others that it takes during its turn.

##### Sudden Block (Su) \[2\]

As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to hinder a melee attack made against you or an adjacent ally. Add your tier to your AC or the ally’s AC against this attack. The creature making the attack must make two [<u>attack rolls</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) and take the lower result. Once the attack is resolved, you or your ally (your choice) can make one melee attack against the creature. The damage from this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Adamantine Mind (Ex) \[1\]

> Your mind is as hard as any [<u>armor</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus), and is dangerous to engage. You gain a bonus equal to your tier on saving throws against [<u>mind-affecting</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Mind-Affecting) effects. Whenever you succeed at a save against a [<u>mind-affecting</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Mind-Affecting) effect, the creature attacking you with that effect must succeed at a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save (at the same DC) or be [<u>stunned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned) for 1 round.

##### Affliction Resistance (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Your body is honed to fight off even the most virulent illnesses. Whenever you succeed at a saving a throw against a [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison), you become immune to that [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison) for a number of hours equal to half your tier. If you are affected by the [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison) and succeed at a saving throw to cure it, you automatically cure yourself of the affliction (even if it would normally require more than one saving throw to cure), and you become immune to it for a number of hours equal to half your tier.

##### Armor Master (Ex) \[1\]

> You don’t take an armor check penalty or incur an arcane spell failure chance when wearing light armor or using a shield (including a tower shield). In addition, the maximum [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) bonus of light armor doesn’t apply to you. You can select this ability up to three times. The second time, it also applies to medium armor. The third time, it also applies to heavy armor.

##### Armored Might (Ex) \[1\]

> You treat the [<u>armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus) from your armor as 50% higher than normal, to maximum increase of half your tier (minimum 1). For example, if you are 4th tier and wearing +2 breastplate, you treat the armor’s +6 [<u>armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus) as +8 (50% of +6 is +3, limited to +2 by half your tier).

##### Avenging Maneuver (Ex) \[1\]

> Taking grievous wounds only empowers you. Any creature that confirms a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) with a melee attack against you provokes an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from you. You can use this [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) only to attempt a [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush), [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm), [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder), or [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check. You don’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) for attempting this [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check.

##### Borrow Elements (Su) \[1\]

> When you take acid, cold, electricity, fire, or sonic damage, you store some of this energy within yourself and can direct it outward through your attacks. Whenever you take energy damage, you gain an amount of energy of that type equal to 1/2 the damage taken (after any resistances or immunities are applied).
>
> Whenever you hit with a melee attack, you may expend 3 points from the pool of one energy type to have the attack deal an additional 1d6 points of energy damage of that type. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to release all stored energy of one type as a ray with a 60-foot range. The ray deals 1d6 points of energy damage for every 3 points of energy stored. If unused, the stored energy harmlessly dissipates 1 minute after the last time you took energy damage of any type.

##### Burst Through (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge), you can move through squares containing allies and opponents almost as if they were not obstructing your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge). You can move through squares containing allies freely, but you must succeed at an [<u>overrun</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Overrun) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check for each opponent that obstructs your path to the target of your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge). If the result of the check exceeds the opponent’s [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense), you may move through the opponent’s square and continue toward the target without provoking an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from that opponent. If you fail any of these [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) checks, your movement ends in the square before that opponent, but you may resolve the [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) attack against the foe that stopped you.

##### Draw Fire (Su) \[1\]

> As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), when an ally within 30 feet is targeted with a ranged weapon attack, you can have that attack target you instead. If you would normally be out of range for that attack, the attack is able to hit you as if you were at maximum range. You can use this ability without using an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) by expending one use of mythic power.

##### Empathic Healing (Su) \[2\]

> You can expend one use of mythic power to transfer up to 20 points of damage per tier from a touched ally to yourself, [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) the target and damaging you. This transferred damage bypasses all effects that reduce or negate hit point damage (such as [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction)). Alternatively, you can expend two uses of mythic power to transfer an ongoing [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison) from a touched ally to yourself, which halts ongoing effects on the target from that affliction and requires you to attempt any remaining saves against it. If you’re immune to the [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison), transferring it to yourself destroys the affliction.

##### Ever Ready (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you make an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), you gain a bonus on the attack and damage rolls equal to your mythic tier. You can make [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) while [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed), even if you don’t have the [<u>Combat Reflexes</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/combat-reflexes-combat) feat. At 3rd, 6th, and 9th tier, the number of [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) you can make each round increases by one.

##### Fast Healing (Ex) \[1\]

> As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to gain [<u>fast healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules/#Fast_Healing_Ex) 5 for 1 minute. This ability can be taken a second time at 3rd tier or higher and a third time at 6th tier or higher. Each additional time you take this ability, the [<u>fast healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules/#Fast_Healing_Ex) increases by 5.

##### Guardian’s Shout (Ex) \[1\]

> Your warning shouts keep allies from danger. Whenever you attempt a [<u>Reflex</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reflex) save to avoid an effect that affects multiple creatures, you can expend one use of your mythic power to call out a warning to your allies. Any allies within 100 feet who can hear you gain a bonus equal to your tier on their saving throws against that effect.

##### Immovable (Su) \[1\]

> Add your tier to your [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense) whenever an enemy attempts a [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush), [<u>drag</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Drag), [<u>reposition</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reposition), or [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against you. If the check is successful, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to cause it to fail instead.

##### Imprinting Hand (Su) \[1\]

> By touching a foe, you can gain knowledge about it, including its weaknesses. To use this ability, you must first successfully hit a foe with an [<u>unarmed strike</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/strike-unarmed), [<u>natural weapon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks), or melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) to make contact, then use this ability is a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions). As long as the target remains within 1 mile of you, you always know the direction and approximate distance to it. If the foe has any weaknesses or [<u>vulnerabilities</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Vulnerabilities-Ex-or-Su-) (including a mythic flaw), you immediately know this information.
>
> You can maintain this connection with only one creature at a time; if you use this ability on another creature, your connection with the previous creature is lost.

##### Indefatigable Traveler (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can run and [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) while [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued) or [<u>exhausted</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Exhausted), and you ignore the movement penalty while [<u>exhausted</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Exhausted). When traveling overland, you can expend one use of mythic power to be able to hustle for a number of hours equal to your tier without taking [<u>nonlethal damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Nonlethal-Damage) or becoming [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued) (this is in addition to the 1 hour per day normally allowed for hustling). In a day of normal walking, you can walk 8 hours plus 1 hour per tier before you must start making [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-) checks to avoid [<u>nonlethal damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Nonlethal-Damage).

##### Irrepressible Soul (Su) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Your true self can’t be buried beneath disguises or magical effects. Once per round as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt to end one effect that is currently affecting you. When you use this ability, you make an additional saving throw against the effect at its original DC, even if one would not normally be allowed. This ability can end only [*<u>polymorph</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/polymorph) effects, [<u>charm and compulsion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Charm-and-Compulsion) effects, domination effects, [<u>illusion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Illusion) effects that change your appearance, and any effects that control or conceal your true identity. If you succeed at the second saving throw, the effect immediately ends.

##### Knowledgeable Guardian (Su) \[1\]

> Add double your tier as a bonus on [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) checks you attempt to identify monsters, including checks made to learn any special powers and [<u>vulnerabilities</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Vulnerabilities-Ex-or-Su-). As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to telepathically communicate the knowledge you obtain from this check to all allies within 100 feet.

##### Lesson Learned (Ex) \[1\]

> You adapt quickly to the perils presented in battle. Whenever you fail a saving throw against a supernatural or [<u>spell-like ability</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-), you gain a +5 bonus on all further saving throws against that ability, including subsequent saves against an ongoing effect, or against the same effect produced by a different source or creature. This bonus lasts for a number of minutes equal to your tier. For example, if you fail a save against a [<u>demon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Demon) [*<u>fireball</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/fireball) spell-like ability, you gain this bonus against a [*<u>fireball</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/fireball) spell-like ability from any creature; if you fail a save against a [<u>blue dragon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/dragons/dragon/chromatic-blue) electricity [<u>breath weapon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Breath-Weapon-Su-), you gain this bonus against all [<u>blue dragon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/dragons/dragon/chromatic-blue) electricity breath weapons, but not against a [<u>black dragon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/dragons/dragon/chromatic-black) acid breath weapon or a [<u>gorgon’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listin/magical-beasts/gorgon) petrification breath weapon. If you fail another saving throw against the same ability, the duration of lesson learned resets but the bonus doesn’t stack with itself.

##### Mule’s Strength (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You treat your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) score as 5 higher when determining your carrying capacity. This bonus applies to encumbrance, the maximum weight you can lift over your head, dragging and pushing, and so on. You can take this path ability multiple times. Each time you do, you treat your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) score as an additional 5 points higher when determining your carrying capacity.

##### Mythic Mercy (Su) \[1\]

> When you use the mercy [<u>paladin</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin) class feature, your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) check to remove curses, diseases, and poisons automatically succeeds unless the affliction is from a mythic source. If you expend one use of mythic power when using a mercy, you add [*<u>break enchantment</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/break-enchantment) to its effect, using your [<u>paladin</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin) level as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level). You must have the [<u>mercy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Mercy-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Pack Wild Shape (Su) \[1\]

> When you use the [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) class feature, you can also transform allies when you [<u>change shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Change-Shape-Su-).
