When you use [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-), you can expend one use of mythic power to select a number of willing allies equal to your tier. These allies take the same [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) form as you do, with the same abilities as yours. Divide the duration of that use of [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) evenly (rounded down) among yourself and the affected allies. The transformation ends for everyone when you return to your normal form or use [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) again. An ally can end its own transformation early as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) without affecting the duration for you or other allies. You must have the [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Partial Transformation (Su) \[1\]

> You have refined control over your transformation ability. When using [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-), you can expend one use of mythic power to only partially transform into or out of [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) form. For example, you could transform your hands into [<u>tiger</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/cat-great/tiger) claws and your head into a [<u>tiger’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/cat-great/tiger) head (giving you [<u>low-light vision</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Low-Light-Vision) and [<u>scent</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Scent), and allowing you to make claw and bite attacks but still wear armor and use worn magic items normally), transform into a [<u>deinonychus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/dinosaur/deinonychus) with [<u>human</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/races/core-races/human) hands (allowing you to still manipulate objects and wear rings), or transform into a [<u>constrictor snake</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/snake/snake-constrictor) with a [<u>humanoid</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Humanoid) head (allowing you to speak and cast spells with verbal [<u>components</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Components)). You may make one such change per round as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) for the duration of that use of [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-).
>
> For example, if you change into a [<u>tiger</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/cat-great/tiger), this ability allows you to partially transform yourself from [<u>tiger</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/cat-great/tiger) form to your normal form and back as needed until the duration ends or you fully return to your normal form. You must have the [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Quick Recovery (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you are [<u>dazed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dazed), [<u>dazzled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dazzled), [<u>deafened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Deafened), [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened), or [<u>stunned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned), reduce the duration of that condition by half (minimum 1 round). Any other effects relating to the source of that condition are unaffected; for example, if you are [<u>panicked</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Panicked) and [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened) from an [*<u>eyebite</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/e/eyebite) spell, the [<u>panicked</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Panicked) effect has the normal duration even though you are [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened) for only half as long. You can select this ability twice. The second time you select it, you also reduce the duration of the [<u>blinded</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Blinded), [<u>nauseated</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Nauseated), and [<u>paralyzed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Paralyzed) conditions by half.

##### Raise Animal (Su) \[2\]

> By burying the corpse of a [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) (at least its bones) and expending one use of mythic power, you raise it from the [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) overnight. You can use this ability on an [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded mount even if it is not a creature of the [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) type. At 1st tier, this functions like [*<u>raise dead</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/raise-dead). At 3rd tier, this functions like [*<u>resurrection</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/resurrection). At 6th tier, this functions like [*<u>true resurrection</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/true-resurrection).

##### Ranged Disarm (Ex) \[1\]

> You can use a ranged attack to [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm) an opponent. Attempt a [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check using your [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) modifier instead of your [<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-) modifier, and add your tier to the roll. Using this ability doesn’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). If your attack is successful, your target drops one item of your choice that it is carrying, even if the item is wielded with two hands. If your attack exceeds the [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense) of the target by 10 or more, the target drops the items it is carrying in both hands (maximum two items if the target has more than two hands). Unlike with a melee [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm), failing the attack by 10 or more doesn’t make you drop your weapon, and you can’t automatically pick up the item dropped.

##### Relentless Healing (Su) \[2\]

> You can restore life to the recently [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead). If a creature has died within 1 round, as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power when casting a [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) spell or using a class feature that heals damage to apply that healing to the [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) creature.
>
> If this healing brings the creature’s [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) above the threshold for death, it comes back to life and stabilizes at its new hit point total (similar to the way [*<u>breath of life</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/breath-of-life) functions); otherwise, it remains [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead). Alternatively, you can expend two uses of mythic power on a [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) creature that would have the ability to magically heal itself if it were alive (such as a [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) [<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) with a prepared [*<u>cure light wounds</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/cure-light-wounds) spell) in order to trigger the most powerful [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) magic it knows or has prepared. If this brings the creature’s [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) above its death threshold, it returns to life.

##### Retributive Reach (Ex) \[1\]

> Treat your reach as 5 feet greater than normal for the purpose of determining whether or not you can make an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). If a creature provokes an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) within this area of increased reach, you can expend one use of mythic power to gain a bonus equal to your tier on the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) and damage roll of the attack of opportunity.

##### Sacrificial Shield (Ex) \[1\]

> Once per round when you would normally be hit by a weapon attack, you can use your shield to block the attack. You must be using a shield in order to use this ability. Subtract your shield’s [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) and [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) from the damage of the attack and apply the remaining damage to your [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points). If the shield takes enough damage to destroy it, it’s destroyed. Otherwise, it gains the [<u>broken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Broken) condition, even if the damage was not enough to give it the [<u>broken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Broken) condition under other circumstances. You can expend one use of mythic power when using this ability to negate any damage dealt to the shield, though you still take any damage that exceeds its [<u>hardness</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/damaging-objects#TOC-Hardness) and [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points). You can choose to negate the damage after the damage is rolled.

##### Supreme Tracker (Su) \[1\]

> If you have something that carries a creature’s scent (such as a personal item or piece of flesh), you can expend one use of mythic power to create a supernatural bond that allows you track that individual.
>
> As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can sense the creature’s general direction and distance, as well as know whether it is alive, [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead), destroyed, [<u>dying</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dying), or [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead). The creature must be on the same plane as you for this ability to function. For the purpose of spells and effects that block [*<u>scrying</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/scrying) and [*<u>divination</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/divination), your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) for this effect is a number equal to double your tier. At 6th tier, you can track the creature if it’s on a different plane than you.

#### 3rd-Tier Guardian Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Cage Enemy (Ex) \[2\]

> You can [<u>pin</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Pinned) enemies foolish enough to face you. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to hinder opponents until the end of your next turn. When this ability is active, any creature moving out of one of your [<u>threatened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Threatened-Squares) squares, even when making a 5-foot step or using a form of movement that doesn’t usually provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), provokes an attack of opportunity from you. If your attack of opportunity hits and deals damage, the creature remains in its current space and its movement ends. Alternatively, you can expend one use of mythic power as part of a charge. If the [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) attack hits, the target can’t move itself from its space until the beginning of your next turn (though others can move the creature).

##### Dimensional Grappler (Su) \[1\]

> When you have an opponent [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled) or [<u>pinned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Pinned) and it attempts to use a [<u>teleportation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Teleportation) effect, you can attempt a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save against the effect, even if it would not normally allow a save. If you succeed, you learn the type of [<u>teleportation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Teleportation) effect (such as [*<u>dimension door</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dimension-door)) and the creature’s intended destination, and then may prevent the effect (as if using a [<u>quickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats/quicken-spell-metamagic) [*<u>dimensional anchor</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dimensional-anchor), using your character level as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level)) or accompany the opponent as if you were part of its gear with negligible weight.

##### Drive Back (Ex) \[1\]

> As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can attempt one [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check and apply it to all opponents within reach. If you have the [<u>Whirlwind Attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/whirlwind-attack-combat) feat, you can use this ability at the same time that you make an attack against each foe within reach, allowing you to attempt a free [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against each foe that you hit.
>
> After you’ve pushed opponents backward, you may move with any one of your [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) targets.

##### Earth Protection (Su) \[2\]

> As long as you’re in contact with earth or unworked stone, you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) to draw energy from the earth and radiate it outward, granting yourself and allies within 30 feet [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 10/[<u>adamantine</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/special-materials#TOC-Adamantine) for 1 minute.

##### Impervious Body (Su) \[1\]

> You gain [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 5/epic. This ability can be taken a second time at 6th tier or higher and a third time at 9th tier or higher. Each additional time it’s taken, the [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) increases by 5.

##### Impervious Companion (Su) \[1\]

> You can make your bonded creature more resistant to damage. Select one [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded mount. This creature gains any epic [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) that you gain from the impervious body path ability. You must have the impervious body path ability to select this ability.

##### Incredible Parry (Ex) \[1\]

> You gain the [<u>duelist’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/prestige-classes/core-rulebook/duelist) parry ability, and you may expend one use of mythic power to add a number equal to your tier to the parry roll. If you already have the duelist’s parry ability, you can use incredible parry and the duelist’s parry class ability in the same round, electing to not make two attacks and instead parrying up to two attacks.

##### Mighty Hurler (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You gain the [<u>rock throwing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rock-Throwing-Ex-) and [<u>rock catching</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rock-Catching-Ex-) universal monster abilities. The range increment of your [<u>rock throwing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Rock-Throwing-Ex-) ability is 10 feet per tier. You can use these abilities with any solid, mostly nonflexible object, not just rocks. The maximum size of an object you can throw is two size categories smaller than you. You can take this ability three times. Each additional time you select it, the range increment increases by an additional 10 feet per tier and the maximum size of an object you can throw increases by one size category.

##### Parry Spell (Su) \[1\]

> As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to block a spell targeting you or an ally adjacent to you. The spell must be a ray, a single-target spell, or a spell that creates an effect targeting one creature (such as [*<u>acid arrow</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/acid-arrow)), and the level of the spell’s spell slot must be equal to or lower than your tier. Make an [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) as if making an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). If the result of the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) is greater than the spell’s [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) or save DC, the spell has no effect on the intended target (though other targets from the same spell, such as multiple targets of [*<u>scorching ray</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/scorching-ray), are affected normally). A spell that has neither a DC nor an [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) (such as [*<u>magic missile</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-missile)) can’t be affected by this ability.
>
> You must declare using spell parry after the spellcaster’s target is announced, but before the target’s saving throw or [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) is made.

##### Possess Companion (Su) \[1\]

> You can expend one use of mythic power to project your soul from your body and into your [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>cohort</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded mount as if possessing that creature with the [*<u>magic jar</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-jar) spell. Unlike with [*<u>magic jar</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/magic-jar), the companion creature’s soul remains within its body, allowing you to know what it knows and share its memories. This effect lasts until you end it as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions).

##### Shrug It Off (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever an enemy confirms a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against you or your [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded mount, you can expend one use of your mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to convert the [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) into a normal hit. Effects that trigger only on a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) don’t trigger when you use this ability to negate the [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits).

##### To the Death (Ex) \[1\]

> You can shrug off wounds that others would find devastating. When below 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), you don’t fall [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious), but are instead [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered). You lose 1 hit point at the end of each turn when you take a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) while [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered) in this way.

##### Turn the Tables (Ex) \[1\]

> If a creature fails a [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check made against you, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt that same maneuver against that creature. You receive a +10 bonus on this [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check.

##### Uncanny Adaptability (Sp) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to create an [<u>invisible</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Invisible) sphere around you that protects anyone inside from harsh environmental effects, as [*<u>planar adaptation</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/planar-adaptation), except this effect also works for extreme physical conditions on the [<u>Material Plane</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/environment/the-planes#TOC-Material-Plane1). The radius of the sphere is equal to 5 feet per tier, and only creatures within the sphere receive the benefits of this effect. Anyone who steps outside of the sphere immediately loses the benefits of this ability until she steps back inside the sphere. This effect lasts for a number of hours equal to your tier or until you end it as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), whichever comes first.

#### 6th-Tier Guardian Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Cling to Life (Su)

> When you die, as long as your body remains, you can be healed by any spell or effect that heals damage, though you regain only half the normal number of [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points). If you’re healed to full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), you come back to life and gain 1 permanent [<u>negative level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Energy-Drain-and-Negative-Levels) for each minute you were [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) (these [<u>negative levels</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Energy-Drain-and-Negative-Levels) can be removed as normal). Until you’re healed to full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), you don’t regain consciousness and are still [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) no matter what other abilities you might have. If you aren’t brought back to life within a number of minutes equal to your total character level, your death is permanent (although you can still be brought back by spells such as [*<u>raise dead</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/raise-dead)).

##### Companion Power (Su)

> You can imbue your bonded creature with your own mythic nature. Select one [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded mount. This creature gains any one guardian path ability as if it were a 1st-tier guardian. If that ability can be used only by expending mythic power, your companion can instead use the ability three times per day.

##### Impassable (Ex)

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You are a beacon of stalwart bravery, and none may pass before you without first being judged. When you use the cage enemy path ability (Mythic Adventures 30) and you successfully deal damage to a creature moving out of one of your [<u>threatened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Threatened-Squares) squares, you may attempt a [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip), [<u>dirty trick</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Dirty-Trick), or [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) against that creature as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) with a +2 [<u>circumstance bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Circumstance-Bonus). This doesn’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). Regardless of whether your maneuver is successful, the creature remains in its current space and its movement ends. You must have the cage enemy guardian path ability to select this ability.

##### Indomitable (Su)

> Select one of the following conditions: [*<u>bleed</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/bleed), [<u>blinded</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Blinded), [<u>confused</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Confused), [<u>dazed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dazed), [<u>deafened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Deafened), [<u>entangled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Entangled), [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued), [<u>frightened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Frightened), [<u>shaken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Shaken), [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened), or [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered). You are immune to that condition. Greater and lesser versions of the selected condition (such as [<u>panicked</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Panicked), a more powerful version of [<u>frightened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Frightened)) still affect you.

##### Invincible Stand (Su)

> You can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) to make yourself nearly invincible for a short period of time. You gain [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 20/— for a number of rounds equal to your tier. If you choose to move or are moved by another creature during this time, this protection immediately ends.

##### Mythic Resolve (Su)

> You’re imbued with power and grace that protects you. Whenever you attempt a saving throw, you can expend one use of mythic power to roll again. You can use this ability more than once and take the highest result of all the rolls. You can use this ability only after the initial roll is made, but before the results are revealed. Using this ability doesn’t require an action, and it can be used as long as you are conscious.

##### Shield of the Martyr (Su)

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> By expending one use of mythic power, you can use the take the hit path ability on an ally within 30 feet rather than an adjacent ally. Alternatively, you can expend one use of mythic power when using take the hit on an adjacent ally to take all of the damage (as well as any other effects, such as [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases), [<u>grab</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Grab-Ex-), or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison)), not just half. This damage is not subject to any resistances or [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) you have. You must have the take the hit guardian path ability to take this ability.

##### Stasis (Su)

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions) to enter a trance that drastically slows your vital functions. You are [<u>helpless</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Helpless) and can’t move or take other actions, nor replenish mythic power, but you age and consume bodily resources as though each year that passes were 1 minute. For example, if you could normally hold your breath for 3 minutes, you can use this ability to survive without air for 3 years. You remain partially aware during this stasis and can detect any lethal threats within 5 feet of you. There are several ways to end this stasis. By expending one use of mythic power as a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can awaken from your stasis at any time. If you take damage while in stasis, you immediately awaken, though you are [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered) for 1 hour. Finally, you can use sheer force of will to awaken from stasis without expending mythic power, taking 1d4 weeks for you to awaken. When activating this ability, you can put an adjacent willing allies into stasis as well. There is no limit to the number of allies you can put into stasis in this way, but you must expend three additional uses of mythic power for each ally.

##### Take the Hit (Ex)

> You are a living shield for your allies.
>
> Whenever an adjacent ally takes damage from a melee or ranged attack, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can split the damage. You take half of the damage (rounded down), and your ally takes the remaining amount. This damage is not subject to any resistances or [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction) you might have, but any such defenses the target of the attack possesses apply before the damage is split. You don’t suffer any other effects from the attack (such as [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases), [<u>grab</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Grab-Ex-), or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison)).

##### Unbreakable Resilience (Ex)

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> When you take this ability, choose either physical ability scores ([<u>Strength</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Strength-Str-), [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-), and [<u>Constitution</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Constitution-Con-)) or mental ability scores ([<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Intelligence-Int-), [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-), and [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-)). You recover [<u>ability damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Ability-Score-Damage) and drain to the selected ability scores at a rate of 1 hour per point of damage rather than 1 day per point. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to recover 1 point of [<u>ability damage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Ability-Score-Damage) to any of the three selected abilities. You can take this ability twice. The second time you select it, unbreakable resilience applies to all six ability scores.

#### 1st-Tier Hierophant Path Abilities

##### Beast’s Fury (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to imbue your [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>cohort</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded mount with some of your mythic power. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), that creature can move up to its speed and make an attack with one of its [<u>natural weapons</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks). When making this attack, the creature rolls twice and takes the higher result. Any damage dealt by this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). A creature affected by this ability can take these actions in addition to any other actions it takes during its turn.

##### Inspired Spell (Su) \[2\]

You can expend one use of mythic power to cast any one divine spell, treating your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) as 2 levels higher. This spell must be on your divine spell list (or your domain or mystery spell list) and must be of a spell level that you can cast using that divine spellcasting class. If you are a spontaneous spellcaster, you don’t need to have the spell prepared, nor does it need to be on your list of spells known. Using this ability does not expend a prepared spell or available spell slot. You can apply any [<u>metamagic feats</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) you know to this spell, but its total spell slot level must be a slot level you can normally cast.

##### Recalled Blessing (Su) \[1\]

You can expend one use of mythic power to cast any one divine spell without expending a prepared spell or spell slot. If you prepare spells, this spell must be one you prepared today; if you’re a spontaneous caster, this spell must be one of your spells known. You can’t apply [<u>metamagic feats</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) to this spell. If the spell requires a saving throw, non-mythic creatures roll twice and take the lower result. If the spell heals damage or requires you to attempt a [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) check to cure an affliction or remove a condition, roll twice and take the higher result.

##### Alter Channel (Su) \[1\]

If you’re in the area of an opponent’s [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability and the energy type is the opposite of yours, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to transform the opponent’s channeled energy to the opposite type. You can cause the altered energy to either affect the opposite kind of targets ([<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead) or living), or change whether it heals or harms, but not both. Channeled energy that has been changed in this way heals or deals half the normal amount of damage.

For example, if an evil [<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) attempts to channel negative energy to heal [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead), you can expend one use of mythic power to convert that to channeled positive energy that either heals the living or harms [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead). You must have the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability to select this ability.

##### Bleed Holy Power (Su) \[1\]

If you [<u>channel positive energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-), when an [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead) creature damages you with a melee attack, you can expend one use of channeled energy as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) to deal damage to [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead) creatures around you (as detailed in the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability). Alternatively, you may focus this channeled energy so it affects only the [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead) creature that hit you.

If you focus solely on that creature, you are considered 4 levels higher in the class that grants you the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) feature when determining its effects.

##### Channel Shockwave (Su) \[1\]

You can expend one use of [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) whenever you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against a creature that has an element of [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) opposite to your own ([<u>chaotic</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Chaotic) and [<u>lawful</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Lawful) are opposite, as are evil and good, so if you were [<u>lawful</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Lawful) good, you could use this ability against a [<u>chaotic</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Chaotic) creature or evil creature). If you do, add your [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) damage to the damage from the [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits). This damage is the same type as your channeled energy (positive or negative) but damages the target regardless of whether it’s living or [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead). The target can attempt a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save against your [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) DC to halve this bonus damage. All other effects of your [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) still apply.

##### Contingent Channel Energy (Su) \[2\]

You can simultaneously expend one use of mythic power and one use of your [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability to delay your [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) so that it comes into effect under some condition you dictate.

The conditions needed to bring the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) into effect must be clear, although they can be general. In all cases, the channel effect occurs instantaneously when the prescribed circumstances occur. For example, you could create a trigger that uses your [<u>channel positive energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) if you are reduced to 0 or fewer [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points). If you prescribe complicated or convoluted conditions, your contingent [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability could fail to trigger. The ability triggers based solely on the stated conditions, regardless of whether you want it to. You make all decisions about the ability when you expend the use of mythic power, according to what you can normally do with your [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability; if you wish to exclude targets, you must name them at that time. You can have only one use of this ability waiting to be triggered at a time; if you use this ability again, the previous use expires. This use also expires after 1 day per tier if it hasn’t been triggered.

This use of [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) can’t be regained until after the contingent [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability is triggered or expires.

##### Divine Countenance (Su) \[1\]

Whenever you encounter a creature whose [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) is within one step of yours, treat its attitude as one step better. If the creature’s starting attitude is helpful, you can make requests of the creature with a +5 bonus on your [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) or [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) skill check.

##### Divine Guardian (Sp) \[1\]

As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) one [<u>extraplanar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Extraplanar) creature as if using a [*<u>summon monster</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-monster) or [*<u>summon nature’s ally</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-natures-ally) spell, with the level of the [<u>summoning</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Summoning) spell equal to half your tier. Your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) for this ability is equal to your character level. You can have only one creature that has been summoned using this ability at a time; [<u>summoning</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Summoning) another causes the previous one to disappear. If you expend two uses of mythic power, the level of the [<u>summoning</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Summoning) spell is equal to your tier instead. For example, if you are a 10th-level [<u>druid</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid), 5th-tier hierophant, you can use this ability to [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) one creature from the [*<u>summon monster II</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-monster) or [*<u>summon nature’s ally II</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-natures-ally) list (or a lower level list) for 10 rounds; if you expend two uses, you can instead [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) one creature from the [*<u>summon monster V</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-monster) or [*<u>summon nature’s ally V</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/summon-natures-ally) list (or a lower-level list).

##### Eldritch Breach (Su) \[1\]

You are adept at breaching magical defenses and resistance to your magic. When attempting a [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) check to dispel an effect, overcome [<u>spell resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Spell-Resistance), or otherwise determine whether or not your magic affects a target (such as with [*<u>knock</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/k/knock) or [*<u>neutralize poison</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/n/neutralize-poison)), roll twice and take the higher result.

##### Empathic Healing (Su) \[2\]

You can expend one use of mythic power to transfer up to 20 points of damage per tier from a touched ally to yourself, [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) the target and damaging you. This transferred damage bypasses all effects that reduce or negate hit point damage (such as [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction)).

Alternatively, you can expend two uses of mythic power to transfer an ongoing [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison) from a touched ally to yourself, which halts ongoing effects on the target from that affliction and requires you to attempt any remaining saves against it. If you’re immune to the [<u>disease</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/diseases) or [<u>poison</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/afflictions/poison), transferring it to yourself destroys the affliction.

##### Endless Bounty (Ex) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

Whenever you cast [*<u>create food and water</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/create-food-and-water), you can continue to cast it indefinitely (subject to [<u>fatigue</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued) and other physical constraints) after the initial 10-minute [<u>casting time</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Casting-Time). For every extra 10 minutes you spend casting the spell, you create enough food to sustain an additional number of [<u>humans</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/races/core-races/human) equal to your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) for 24 hours. At 6th tier, you may also use this ability when you cast [*<u>heroes’ feast</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/heroes-feast).

##### Enhance Magic Items (Ex) \[1\]

Your mythic presence enhances the power of certain magic items. Add half your tier to the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) of [*<u>potions</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions), [*<u>scrolls</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/scrolls), staves, and [*<u>wands</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands) you use. When using a staff or [*<u>wand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands), you may [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) the item by expending one use of mythic power instead of one of the item’s charges.

##### Faith’s Reach (Su) \[1\]

Whenever you cast a divine spell with a range of [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks), you can instead cast the spell with a range of 30 feet. If the spell normally requires a melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks), it instead requires a ranged [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks).

##### Flexible Counterspell (Su) \[1\]

Your mythic power enhances your ability to counter spells. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt to [<u>counter</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Counterspells) a spell. Upon using this ability, you must identify the spell with a [<u>Spellcraft</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/spellcraft) check (DC 15 + the spell’s level). If you correctly identify the spell, you can expend a spell or spell slot of a level equal to or higher than the spell being cast to counter the spell.

##### Heathen Slayer (Ex) \[1\]

Pick one deity (or similar entity able to grant divine spells to followers) as your religious rival. You gain a +2 bonus on weapon attack and damage rolls against this deity’s followers, as well as a +2 bonus on [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff), [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge), [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception), [<u>Sense Motive</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sense-motive), and [<u>Survival</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/survival) checks attempted against this deity’s followers.

At 6th tier, these bonuses increase to +4. You can select this ability multiple times. Each time you do, choose an additional deity to be a religious rival.

##### Impeccable Intuition (Ex) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

Choose one of the following: concealed information, concealed emotion, or secret messages. When talking or dealing with a non-mythic intelligent creature, you always detect the selected type of information without a skill check, though you do not necessarily understand what the information means.

For example, realizing that someone is concealing an emotion doesn’t necessarily tell you what emotion the person is hiding, whether she’s lying, or why. This ability has no effect against mythic creatures. You can select this path ability up to three times. Each additional time you take it, you must choose an additional option.

##### Insightful Interaction (Ex) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

Add half your tier to [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-)-based skill checks in which you are trained.

Additionally, by expending one use of mythic power, you can use your [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-) modifier plus half your tier in place of your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier for [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff), [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy), and [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) checks for 1 hour.

##### Instrument of Faith (Su) \[1\]

You gain [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 15/— against attacks made with your deity’s favored weapon. You can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) to attempt a [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm) or [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against each opponent within 60 feet that is wielding your deity’s favored weapon. You decide individually whether to [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm) or [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) each opponent’s weapon. Attempt one [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check, using your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) in place of your [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus) and adding your tier to the total, and use that result against all opponents.

##### Inverted Spontaneous Casting (Su) \[1\]

If you can spontaneously cast cure or inflict spells, you gain the ability to spontaneously cast the opposite type of spell (i.e., you can spontaneously cast inflict spells if you can normally cast cure spells, or cures spell if you can normally cast inflict spells).

##### Mighty Summons (Su) \[2\]

Each creature you conjure with any [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) spell gains [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 5/epic for the duration of the [<u>summoning</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Summoning). If you cast a [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) spell to [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) more than one creature, you [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) one additional creature of the same type. If you cast a [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) spell to [<u>summon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Summon-Sp-) just one creature, you can expend one use of mythic power to give it the [*<u>agile</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/magic-weapons/magic-weapon-special-abilities/agile) or savage mythic simple template for the duration of the [<u>summoning</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Summoning).

##### Mythic Domain (Su) \[1\]

When determining the effects of your domain’s granted powers, you’re considered 4 levels higher. This increases the effects of powers you have access to, but doesn’t grant you powers at a lower level than normal. Once per day, you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) to regain the use of all your domain granted powers as if you had rested for 8 hours.

If you’re an [<u>oracle</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/oracle), all aspects of this ability apply to your revelations instead of to domain granted powers. You must have the domain class feature or mystery class feature to select this ability.

##### Mythic Wild Shape (Su) \[1\]

Choose one form you can take using [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-). Using [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) to take this form doesn’t count against your daily uses of [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-). You can select this ability multiple times. Each time you select it, choose another form to not count against your daily uses of [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-). You must have the [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Overflowing Grace (Su) \[1\]

When you heal a creature with positive energy (such as by channeling positive energy to heal the living, using cure spells, or using [<u>lay on hands</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Lay-On-Hands-Su-)), any affected creature at full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) (whether already at full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or healed to full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) by your channeled energy) gains a +1 [<u>sacred bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Sacred-Bonus) on [<u>attack rolls</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll), skill checks, ability checks, and saving throws for 1 minute. If you [<u>channel negative energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) to heal [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead), this is a [<u>profane bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Profane-Bonus) rather than a [<u>sacred bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Sacred-Bonus).

##### Pack Wild Shape (Su) \[1\]

Whenever you use the [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) class feature, you can also transform allies when you [<u>change shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Change-Shape-Su-). When you use [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-), you can expend one use of mythic power to select a number of willing allies equal to your tier. These allies take the same [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) form as you do, with the same abilities as yours. Divide the duration of that use of [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) evenly (rounded down) among yourself and the affected allies.

The [*<u>transformation</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/transformation) ends for everyone when you return to your normal form or use [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) again. An ally can end its own [*<u>transformation</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/transformation) early as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) without affecting the duration for you or other allies. You must have the [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) class feature to select this ability.

##### Plantbringer (Su) \[1\]

All plants within a 1-mile radius of you grow at double their normal rate and don’t suffer from any diseases or maladies. Furthermore, allied [<u>plant</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Plant) creatures within 30 feet of you gain [<u>fast healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules/#Fast_Healing_Ex) equal to your tier. If you use [*<u>plant shape</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/plant-shape-i) or [<u>wild shape</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Shape-Su-) to take the form of a [<u>plant</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Plant), you gain this [<u>fast healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules/#Fast_Healing_Ex) in [<u>plant</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Plant) form.

##### Relentless Healing (Su) \[2\]

You can restore life to the recently [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead). If a creature has died within 1 round, as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to apply [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) magic to that creature. This [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) can be from a spell or effect you cause or from a magic item you wield. If this [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) brings the creature’s [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) above the threshold for death, it comes back to life and stabilizes at its new hit point total (similar to the way [*<u>breath of life</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/breath-of-life) functions); otherwise, it remains [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead). Alternatively, you can expend two uses of mythic power on a [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) creature that would have the ability to magically [*<u>heal</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/heal) itself if it were alive (such as a [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) [<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) with a prepared [*<u>cure light wounds</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/cure-light-wounds) spell) in order to trigger the most powerful [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) magic it knows or has prepared. If this brings the creature’s [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) above its death threshold, it returns to life.

##### Sustained by Faith (Su) \[1\]

You require no food, water, or [*<u>sleep</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/sleep). If you have abilities or class features that require rest before they can be regained, you can choose to regain them once per day by spending 1 hour in uninterrupted meditation. If you are 3rd tier or higher, you can expend one use of mythic power in order to also not need to breathe for 24 hours.

##### Symbol of the Holy (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can create a wooden [<u>holy symbol</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/goods-and-services/tools-and-skill-kits#TOC-Holy-Symbol-Silver-or-Wooden) dedicated to your deity. If it ever leaves your possession, it fades away after 1 hour.

Whenever you use this [<u>holy symbol</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/goods-and-services/tools-and-skill-kits#TOC-Holy-Symbol-Silver-or-Wooden) as a divine focus when casting a spell, you don’t need to have any other material component, unless that component costs more than 50 gp per tier you possess.

##### Tongue of the Land (Su) \[1\]

[<u>Animals</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal), [<u>elementals</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Elemental), [<u>fey</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Fey), and plants are magically able to understand you when you speak Druidic, as if you were using [*<u>speak with animals</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/speak-with-animals) or [*<u>tongues</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/tongues). If speaking to creatures of different types at the same time, you can allow all of them to understand you or speak understandably to just one type at a time. For example, if speaking to a group of [<u>animals</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) and [<u>fey</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Fey), you can make yourself understandable to all of them, just to the [<u>animals</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal), or just to the [<u>fey</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Fey).

##### Water of Life (Su) \[2\]

Any [*<u>potions</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) you consume have the maximum effect, as if they were created using the [<u>Maximize Spell</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats/maximize-spell-metamagic) feat. You can quickly create a temporary [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) by casting a spell into a pint of water. The spell must be a valid choice for the [<u>Brew Potion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/item-creation-feats/brew-potion-item-creation) feat and have a spell level no higher than your tier. The targeted liquid becomes a [*<u>potion of</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) that spell. If not consumed, the temporary [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) reverts to normal water after 1 hour per tier.

#### 3rd-Tier Hierophant Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Abundant Healing (Su) \[1\]

When you use a spell or class ability to heal a creature’s hit point damage, you can apply excess [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) (over the target’s full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points)) to one ally adjacent to you. You can continue to apply excess [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) to other adjacent allies until no injured allies are adjacent or you run out of excess [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing). For example, if you could cure 40 points of damage with your [*<u>cure critical wounds</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/cure-critical-wounds) spell and the target has taken only 20 points of damage, you can apply the remaining 20 points of [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) to an adjacent ally; if that second ally has taken only 15 points of damage, you can apply the remaining 5 points of [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) to another adjacent ally.

##### Alignment Insight (Ex) \[1\]

You can automatically detect the [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) of any [<u>outsider</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Outsider) you can see. This ability doesn’t function if the creature is magically disguised or has been polymorphed into a different shape. You can automatically detect the [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) of any creature you see casting a divine spell; if that creature worships a deity, you also learn its deity. Spells and effects that mask a creature’s [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) prevent the use of this ability. Whenever you cast a spell that affects creatures differently based on their [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) (such as [*<u>holy smite</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/holy-smite)), you may have the spell treat creatures of one particular [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) as an [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment) one step away from that [<u>alignment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Alignment). For example, when casting [*<u>holy smite</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/holy-smite), you could treat all lawful neutral creatures as [<u>lawful</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Lawful) good or lawful evil, all [<u>chaotic</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Chaotic) neutral creature as [<u>chaotic</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Chaotic) good or chaotic evil, or all neutral creatures as neutral good or neutral evil.

##### Animal Friend (Ex) \[1\]

If you have the [<u>wild empathy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Empathy-Ex-) ability, you can use it as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) once per round. If you don’t have that ability, you can instead attempt [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) checks (with a –8 penalty) to improve the attitude of [<u>animals</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) as if they were intelligent creatures. Add your tier to [<u>wild empathy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Wild-Empathy-Ex-) or [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) checks to influence [<u>animals</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal).

##### Blessed Companion (Su) \[1\]

Select one [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), [<u>familiar</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/monster-listings/animals/familiar), or bonded [*<u>mount</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mount). It gains [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) 5/epic.

This ability can be taken a second time at 6th tier or higher and a third time at 9th tier or higher. Each additional time it’s taken, the [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) increases by 5.

##### Divine Metamastery (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power and pick any one [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) you know that increases the slot level of the spell by 0 levels or 1 level. For the next 10 rounds, you can apply this [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) to any divine spell you cast without increasing the spell slot used or [<u>casting time</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Casting-Time). You can also use this ability on a divine spell cast from a [*<u>scroll</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/scrolls), staff, or [*<u>wand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands). You can’t have more than one use of this ability active at a time. If you use this ability again, any previous use immediately ends (though any [<u>metamagic</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) effects on spells already cast remain). You can select this ability more than once. Each time you select it, the maximum spell slot level increase of the [<u>metamagic feat</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/metamagic-feats) goes up by 1 to a maximum of 4.

##### Divine Potency (Su) \[1\]

Choose one of your divine spellcasting classes. You gain four bonus 1st-level divine spell slots for that class (like the bonus spells gained from a high ability score). This doesn’t change your number of spells known. You can select this ability more than once. If you choose the same divine spellcasting class a second time, you gain four bonus 2nd-level spell slots; if you choose it again, you gain 3rd-level slots for choosing it a third time, and so on. This ability can’t grant you bonus spells for a spell level that you don’t have the ability to cast.

##### Domain Immunity (Su) \[2\]

You don’t take damage or suffer harmful effects from spells on your domain spell list, even ones you aren’t of a high enough level to cast.

If you’re targeted by any of these spells, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to turn the spell back upon the original caster as if using [*<u>spell turning</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/spell-turning). Beneficial effects of spells on your domain spell list affect you normally. If you’re an [<u>oracle</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/oracle), this ability applies to your mystery’s bonus spells. You must have the domain class feature or mystery class feature.

##### Enduring Blessing (Su) \[1\]

Whenever you cast a spell with a duration of 10 minutes per level or longer upon one willing target, you can change that spell’s duration to 24 hours. If the spell has other duration conditions, those still apply (for example, the duration of [*<u>stoneskin</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/stoneskin) changes to 24 hours or until discharged). A creature can’t be subject to more than one spell affected by this ability at a time; if another is cast upon the creature, the first one ends. You can select this ability a second time at 3th tier or higher. The second time you select it, you can use it on spells with a duration of 1 minute per level or longer.

##### Flowers in Your Footsteps (Sp) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to radiate an aura of [*<u>plant growth</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/plant-growth). Vegetation within 60 feet of you grows rapidly, as though you had cast the overgrowth version of [*<u>plant growth</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/plant-growth) ([<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) equal to your character level). This ability lasts for 1 minute per tier, and follows you wherever you move until the duration expires or you end the effect as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions). You are immune to the effects of this overgrowth, as are any allies you designate upon using this ability, up to a maximum number of creatures equal to your tier.

##### Hand of Mercy (Su) \[1\]

If you have the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability, you can [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) a creature and expend one use of [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) to remove a condition afflicting that creature. Each time you use this ability, you can choose one of the following conditions: [<u>dazed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dazed), [<u>fatigued</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Fatigued), [<u>shaken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Shaken), [<u>sickened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Sickened), or [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered). At 6th tier, you can also choose from among the cursed, [<u>exhausted</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Exhausted), [<u>frightened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Frightened), and [<u>nauseated</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Nauseated) conditions.

At 9th tier, you can also choose from among the [<u>blinded</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Blinded), [<u>deafened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Deafened), [<u>paralyzed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Paralyzed), and [<u>stunned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned) conditions.
