##### Hear the Word (Su) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

When you cast a spell such as [*<u>augury</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/augury) that gives you information directly from a divine source, you can expend one use of mythic power to reduce the [<u>casting time</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Casting-Time) to a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) and have the answer be audible in the unmistakable voice of your deity, granting you a bonus equal to your tier on [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) and [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) checks against those who hear the voice for a number of hours equal to your tier. If you expend one additional use of mythic power, the voice deals 1d6 points of sonic damage per tier to enemies within 60 feet of you who can hear it, and they are [<u>stunned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned) for 1 round.

A successful [<u>Fortitude</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Fortitude) save (DC = 10 + your tier + your [<u>Wis</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-) modifier) halves the damage and negates the [<u>stun</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned) effect.

##### Hurling Vengeance (Su) \[1\]

Whenever you are wielding your deity’s favored weapon or your [<u>paladin</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin) bonded weapon, you can throw it as if it had the [*<u>returning</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/returning-weapon) and throwing weapon special abilities. If using a [<u>paladin</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin) bonded weapon, it retains its bonded abilities when thrown this way. By expending one use of mythic power as part of a full attack, the weapon returns to you immediately after each time you throw it (instead of just before your next turn) allowing you to make your full number of attacks that round.

##### Life Current (Su) \[0,5\]

You are more attuned to the source of life or unlife and better able to access its power. When casting a cure or inflict spell or use a class feature that uses positive or negative energy (such as [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) or [<u>lay on hands</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Lay-On-Hands-Su-)), treat any natural 1s on the dice rolled to heal or deal damage as 2s. You can select this ability up to four times. The second time you select it, treat natural 1s and 2s as 3s. The third time you select it, treat natural 1s, 2s, and 3s as 4s. The fourth time, treat natural 1s, 2s, 3s, and 4s as 5s.

##### Pilgrim of the Waves (Su) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You can breathe water as easily as you breathe air. You can also walk on any body of water, as [*<u>water walk</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/w/water-walk), except this ability does not allow you to walk upon any bodies of liquid other than water.

You can choose to sink to the bottom of any body of water you could walk upon, descending at a rate of 60 feet per round, and thereafter you can travel along the bottom of the water as though it were dry land. While at the bottom of a body of water, you can attack and act normally, as though under the effects of a [*<u>freedom of movement</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/freedom-of-movement) spell, though this ability does not impart the other benefits of that spell.

##### Shape Channel (Ex) \[1\]

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can simultaneously expend one use of mythic power and one use of your [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) ability to [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) in a 60-foot cone, a 20-foot-tall cylinder with a 20-foot radius, or a 120-foot line. In addition, whenever you [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) to damage creatures (whether [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead) creatures with positive energy or living creatures with negative energy), you can choose to deal half damage (or one-quarter damage to creatures that succeed at their saving throws) and then push back all creatures damaged by the effect. You push each creature back 5 feet for every 1d6 points of [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) damage you are capable of dealing. Creatures that succeed at their saving throws against your channeled energy are only pushed back half as far (minimum 5 feet). You must have the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) class feature to select this path ability.

#### 6th-Tier Hierophant Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Arcane [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) (Ex)

You can use divine power to cast a small number of arcane spells. Choose three 1stlevel spells from the [<u>sorcerer/wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/spell-lists-and-domains/spell-lists-sorcerer-and-wizard/) spell list or three 1st-level spells from the [<u>bard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard) spell list. You can add those spells to your list of spells that you can prepare each day.

You can select this ability up to three times. Each time you select it, you choose three more spells from the same class’s spell list ([<u>sorcerer/wizard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/spell-lists-and-domains/spell-lists-sorcerer-and-wizard/) or [<u>bard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard)), and the highest spell level you can select from increases by 1 (up to 2nd level the second time and up to 3rd level the third time).

You must be a [<u>cleric</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric) or [<u>druid</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid) to select this ability.

##### Conduit of Divine Will (Su)

Your mythic nature is directly connected to the divine. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to use [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-), use [<u>lay on hands</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Lay-On-Hands-Su-), or cast any one spell from your domain spell lists (as long as you can normally prepare a spell of that level). Whenever you use this ability, you also gain [<u>spell resistance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Spell-Resistance) equal to 10 + your tier and a +4 [<u>sacred bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Sacred-Bonus) (or [<u>profane bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Profane-Bonus) if you’re evil) to your AC for a number of rounds equal to your tier.

##### Overcome Curse (Su)

You are immune to curse effects.

For example, [*<u>bestow curse</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/bestow-curse) has no effect on you, and you can handle, carry, or discard a cursed magic item without [*<u>harm</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/harm). If a cursed item has useful functions and a cursed effect (such as armor of arrow attraction) or drawback (such as a mace of blood), you are immune to the cursed effect and drawback, and can still use its other abilities. If a cursed item works normally for a period of time before its curse triggers (such as gauntlets of fumbling), the item stops working for you when the curse would normally trigger. If you’re an [<u>oracle</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/oracle), you can ignore the negative effects of your [<u>oracle’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/oracle) curse.

##### Puppet Master (Su)

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can temporarily raise a [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) creature within 60 feet to serve as your puppet for up to a number of rounds equal to your tier or until you end this effect as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), whichever comes first. In order to use this ability, you must expend a number of uses of mythic power equal to half the target’s [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-). The creature must have been slain within a number of rounds equal to your tier. You imbue the creature with enough positive or negative energy to allow its body to function without its spirit.

This shell of a creature gains the mindless quality, and it acts like a puppet with you as the puppeteer.

The creature is neither living nor [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead), and positive and negative energy have no effect on the creature. It immediately regains half its maximum number of [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points), which becomes its new maximum hit point total for the duration of this effect. You can command the puppet as though it were under the effects of a [*<u>dominate monster</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/dominate-monster) spell cast by you (no save), and it can use any of the abilities it normally had access to at the time of death (prepared spells, special abilities, and so on). The puppet must still have all the necessary [<u>components</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Components) to [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) special abilities and spells. Abilities that normally refresh periodically, like breath weapons, do not refresh. Once the puppet takes an amount of damage equal to its total number of [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or the duration of this effect ends, the puppet is destroyed and becomes a corpse once again. The puppet can be affected as normal by [*<u>resurrection</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/r/resurrection) spells and effects during this time, which immediately end the effects of this ability. You can’t affect the same creature with this ability more than once in 24 hours. You must have the [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) class feature to select this path ability.

##### Pilgrim of the Sky (Su)

**Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)

You can walk on clouds as easily as flat land, and are never affected by altitude-based effects such as altitude sickness. If you are on a mountain or other geographically high location, you can simply step from the earth onto the clouds. Otherwise, you must find your own way to the clouds overhead if you wish to walk on them. This ability only works when there are clouds present; you cannot walk on clear skies.

You can expend one use of mythic power to use [*<u>air walk</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/air-walk) ([<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) equal to your character level). You may expend one additional point of mythic power to grant the same ability to an ally touching you. There is no limit to the number of allies you can affect with this ability. At 9th tier, you can expend one use of mythic power to use [*<u>overland flight</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/o/overland-flight).

##### Servant of Balance (Su)

Whenever a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) is confirmed against you, the attack deals normal damage instead of critical damage. Other effects that trigger on a successful [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) (such as the [<u>Blinding Critical</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/blinding-critical-combat-critical) feat, the fire damage from a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) with a [*<u>flaming burst</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/magic-weapons/magic-weapon-special-abilities/flaming-burst) weapon, or the effects of a [*<u>vorpal</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/magic-weapons/magic-weapon-special-abilities/vorpal) weapon) all affect you normally.

##### Undying Healer (Su)

Whenever you fall [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious), a ghostly image of you manifests from out of your body. Even though you’re [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious), you control this image. On your turn each round, this image can fly up to 30 feet and use your ability to [<u>lay on hands</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Lay-On-Hands-Su-) to heal, [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) to heal, or cast any [<u>conjuration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration) ([<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing)) spell that you could cast. This consumes these abilities as normal. Abilities used by the image can’t benefit you.

This image lasts a number of rounds equal to your tier. This ability can be dispelled, using double your tier as the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level). If you die or regain consciousness, the image immediately ends.

#### 1st-Tier Marshal Path Abilities

##### Advance (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to inspire a tactical advance on the field of battle. This allows you and each of your allies within 30 feet to take either a single [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions) of the character’s choice or a 5-foot step, as long as you or your ally has the ability to take such an action (for example, an [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious) character still can’t take an action). The action granted by this ability doesn’t count toward the number or type of actions a creature is allowed to take on its turn.

##### Decisive Strike (Su) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to give one ally within 30 feet the ability to immediately make a single melee or ranged attack on your turn. Add your tier as a bonus on the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll). The damage dealt by this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). This attack doesn’t count toward the ally’s actions on its turn.

##### Rally (Su) \[1\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to rally your allies. This gives you and each of your allies within 30 feet the option to reroll any one d20 roll after the roll is made. The granted reroll must be used before the beginning of your next turn, and the creature must take the result of the reroll, even if it is lower.

##### Assured Skill (Ex) \[1\]

> In times of need, you’re virtually assured success when using skills. As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) before you attempt a skill check, you can expend one use of mythic power to roll that skill check twice, take the higher result, and add your tier to the total.

##### Clarion Call (Ex) \[2\]

> You bestow your wrath on your allies. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to grant all allies within 30 feet the benefits of your active [<u>smite</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) or challenge ability, directed at the same opponent you’re targeting with that ability. Only the constant effects from the [<u>smite</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) or challenge are conferred with this ability, not the ones you can use only once. This ability lasts a number of rounds equal to your tier or until either the combat ends or the subject of the [<u>smite</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) or challenge is [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead) or [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious).

##### Commanding Entrance (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> When you first enter an area or make your presence known to a group of strangers, as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power and attempt a single [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) or [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check to modify the attitudes of everyone within 10 feet per tier, and an additional number of characters equal to your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier who are outside that range but have line of sight to you. (For instance, you could affect everyone near the entrance of a throne room and key people on the dais at the far end.) Alternatively, when you reveal yourself during the first round of combat (not counting the [<u>surprise</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Surprise) round), you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) to use [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) to demoralize your targets.

##### Deadly Guidance (Ex) \[1\]

> You are able to point out the weak points in an enemy’s defenses, and your allies benefit from your tactical insight. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to designate a single opponent within 30 feet of you. All allies within 30 feet of that opponent gain the [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) ability when attacking it, and can thus deal +1d6 points of damage when [<u>flanking</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Flanking) that opponent or when the opponent is otherwise denied its [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) bonus to AC. This [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage stacks with [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage from other sources. This ability lasts a number of rounds equal to half your tier (minimum 1). You can take this ability up to four times. Each time you take it, the [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage from this ability increases by 1d6.

##### Directed Assault (Ex) \[2\]

> By finding a gap in your enemy’s defenses, you allow your allies to take advantage of your discovery. When you confirm a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) with a melee or ranged weapon against an opponent, allies within 30 feet of that opponent add your tier to their critical confirmation rolls against that opponent. This bonus lasts for a number of rounds equal to your tier. During this duration, you can expend one use of mythic power to convert an ally’s successful attack into a critical threat.
>
> The ally must be within 30 feet, and can also gain the bonus to its confirmation roll. Only one opponent can be the focus of your directed assault at a time. If you confirm a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against another creature, you may make it the new focus (immediately ending the previous focus) or maintain the current focus.

##### Distracting Assailant (Ex) \[2\]

> You make a barrage of attacks and quick feints that are so distracting your opponent loses focus against all other assailants. Whenever you successfully strike a foe with a melee weapon or [<u>natural attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Natural-Attacks), you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions). For 1 round, that opponent loses its [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) bonus to AC on all attacks against it other than yours.
>
> While this ability is in effect, any allies who [<u>flank</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Flanking) that foe gain a +4 bonus on [<u>attack rolls</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) against it, instead of the normal +2. While the opponent is affected by this ability, each time you hit it, the duration increases by 1 round. This effect automatically ends if your turn ends and the target is not within your melee reach.

##### Granted Stride (Ex) \[1\]

> Your mythic grace means that you and your allies easily pass through undergrowth at full speed. Allies within 30 feet of you gain the benefits of [<u>woodland stride</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Woodland-Stride-Ex-) (as the [<u>ranger</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger) class feature), as long as they can see and hear you. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to allow allies within 30 feet to ignore naturally occurring difficult terrain in any of your favored terrains for 1 minute per tier. You must have the [<u>woodland stride</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Woodland-Stride-Ex-) and [<u>favored terrain</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger#TOC-Favored-Terrain-Ex-) class features to select this ability.

##### Helpful Rebuke (Ex) \[1\]

> You are quick with stern yet helpful words of advice. Whenever an ally within line of sight fails a skill check with a skill you’re trained in, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to allow that ally to reroll that skill check with a bonus equal to your tier. If the new roll is successful, your ally succeeds at that check instead of failing it. The ally who failed the skill check must be able to see and hear you to gain this reroll. You can use this ability with a skill you aren’t trained in, but you must expend two uses of mythic power to do so, and the ally doesn’t gain your tier as a bonus on the reroll.

##### Heroic Block (Ex) \[2\]

> With surprising speed, you can leap to an ally’s defense. When a opponent makes a melee attack against one of your allies, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to move up to double your speed or take a 5-foot step. You must end this movement adjacent to the attacking opponent. When you end this movement, you can immediately use the [<u>aid another</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Aid-Another) action to grant a bonus to your ally’s AC (Core Rulebook 197). This bonus is applied before the opponent’s triggering attack. These actions don’t count against your actions this turn.

##### Inspire Minions (Ex) \[1\]

> When you use a spell, a [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance), or another class feature to grant a morale or [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus), the bonus increases by 2 for creatures with at least 4 [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-) fewer than you. For example, if you’re a 6th-level [<u>bard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard) using [<u>inspire courage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Inspire-Courage-Su-), creatures with 2 [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-) or fewer receive a +4 [<u>morale bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Morale-Bonus) on [<u>fear</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Fear) saves and a +4 [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus) on attack and weapon damage rolls instead of +2.

##### Inspired Defense (Ex) \[2\]

> Whenever you use [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) to [<u>inspire courage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Inspire-Courage-Su-), the [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus) against [<u>charm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Enchantment-Charm) and fear effects instead applies to all saving throws. If you expend one use of mythic power when you start a [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) to [<u>inspire courage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Inspire-Courage-Su-), you add your tier to this bonus.

##### Lightning Performance (Ex) \[1\]

> Your tongue is as quick as any blade. You can start a [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) instead of a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions). If you expend one use of mythic power, you can instead start a [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions).

##### Loyalty (Ex) \[2\]

> You gain [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) as a bonus feat. If you already have the [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) feat (or if you take it at a later level), add your tier to your [<u>Leadership</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) score. Whenever you use your marshal’s order ability and your [<u>cohort</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) is within 100 feet, you can include your [<u>cohort</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/leadership) as an additional ally affected by your order.

##### Menacing Presence (Su) \[1\]

> Something about you puts others on edge. You can attempt an [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check to influence a creature’s attitude as [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions) instead of it taking 1 minute. In combat, you can attempt an [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check to demoralize an opponent as a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions). If you expend one use of mythic power, you can attempt to use [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) to demoralize all enemies within 30 feet as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), or as a Swift [<u>action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions) if using the [<u>Dazzling Display</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/combat-feats/dazzling-display-combat) feat.

##### Mounted Marshal (Ex) \[2\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Whenever you’re riding on a bonded [*<u>mount</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mount) or [<u>animal companion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid/animal-companions), you and your mount are each treated as [<u>flanking</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Flanking) any opponent you both threaten. Whenever you use your surge ability while mounted, your mount heals a number of [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) equal to your surge result plus your tier. You can expend one use of mythic power while charging with your mount to deal double damage to the target of your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) (or triple damage if you’re wielding a [<u>lance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/lance)).

##### Mythic Fascination (Su) \[1\]

> When you use the [<u>fascinate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Fascinate-Su-) or [*<u>suggestion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/suggestion) [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) ability, non-mythic creatures take a penalty on their saving throws against it equal to half your tier. If you expend one use of mythic power when you [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) the performance, the penalty also applies to mythic creatures.

##### Painful Gambit (Ex) \[1\]

> Facing off against you is never a safe option when your allies stand beside you. When you are hit by an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to allow all of your allies to make an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) against the creature that attacked you if it’s within their melee reach.

##### Perfect Aid (Ex) \[2\]

> Whenever you successfully use the [<u>aid another</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Aid-Another) action, your ally also adds your tier to the [<u>aid another</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Aid-Another) bonus. If you use your surge ability on the [<u>aid another</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Aid-Another) roll, add the result of the surge die to this bonus as well.

##### Persuasive Countenance (Su) \[1\]

> Whenever you encounter a creature whose attitude is at least indifferent to you, treat its attitude as one step better. If the creature’s starting attitude is helpful, you can make requests of the creature with a +5 bonus on your [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) or [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check.

##### Potent Message (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can expend one use of mythic power to create a song, oration, poem, or joke that contains information that you want to spread.
>
> This can include points of fact or simple instructions.
>
> You only need to perform the work once (which takes 1d4 hours to complete); thereafter it will spread through the community by itself. For 1 day per tier, all non-mythic beings that hear this message (whether from you or someone else) are compelled to spread it to others by slipping it naturally into conversation or otherwise bringing it up. Anyone who has heard this message takes a –4 penalty on opposed [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy), [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate), or [<u>Sense Motive</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sense-motive) checks against you when you request that they to carry out duties related to this message. This is a [<u>language-dependent</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Language-Dependent) effect. At 6th tier or higher, you can expend one additional use of mythic power to make this work universally understandable, regardless of any language barriers.

##### Press the Advantage (Ex) \[1\]

> When an ally seriously harms a foe, you seize the moment. When an ally within 30 feet confirms a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against a creature you threaten, the target of the [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) provokes an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from you. If you hit with your [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), all of your allies gain a +2 bonus on [<u>attack rolls</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) against that creature for 1 round.

##### Redirect Attention (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Where you point, others feel overwhelmingly compelled to look. You can expend one use of mythic power to direct the attention of a number of creatures equal to your tier within line of sight toward a location or general direction you choose—and away from others; non-mythic creatures automatically turn toward the point you choose. Mythic targets receive a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) saving throw to resist this effect (DC = 10 + your tier + your [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-) modifier). The effect persists for 1 round per tier, during which time affected targets receive a +5 bonus on [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) checks to notice anything unusual within a 60-foot-cone in the direction you indicate and a –15 penalty on [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) checks to notice anything elsewhere. This effect counts as a diversion to hide for creatures attempting to use [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) in any area other than the location you directed attention toward.

##### Resurging Words (Su) \[1\]

> Using the power of your mythic presence, you spur on allies even when all seems lost.
>
> Using this ability takes 10 uninterrupted minutes, during which time you give a stirring speech to all allies within 30 feet of you. If you expend one use of mythic power at the end of the speech, your allies gain a number of [<u>temporary hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Temporary-Hit-Points) equal to your tier plus your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier, and a bonus on saving throws against fear equal to half your tier (minimum 1). Any ally who currently has 1/4 of its full [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or fewer gains twice as many [<u>temporary hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Temporary-Hit-Points) from this ability. The [<u>temporary hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Temporary-Hit-Points) and saving throw bonus last for 24 hours. Allies can benefit from this ability only once every 24 hours.

##### Rise Up (Su) \[1\]

> Your allies draw strength from your courage and leadership. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to grant all of your non-mythic allies within 30 feet one use of the surge ability, using your surge die. This surge must be used within a number of rounds equal to your tier. An ally can benefit from this ability only once every 24 hours.

##### Shout of Defiance (Su) \[1\]

> You can channel holy energy into a righteous shout of defiance. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power and one use of either [<u>channel energy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/cleric#TOC-Channel-Energy-Su-) or [<u>lay on hands</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Lay-On-Hands-Su-) to create a 100-foot spread of [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) energy. All allies within this spread heal a number of [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) equal to 1d6 plus double your tier. If this brings an ally from negative [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) to 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or more, the ally can immediately stand up from [<u>prone</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Prone) without provoking an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity).

##### Smiting Aura (Su) \[2\]

> Whenever you use your [<u>smite evil</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) ability, you can expend one use of your mythic power to create a 20-foot-radius aura centered on you that lasts for a number of rounds equal to your tier. Any evil creature that starts its turn in or moves into this area is affected by your smite evil ability. You must have the [<u>smite evil</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-) class ability to select this ability.

##### Stand Tall (Su) \[1\]

> Before you attempt a saving throw against a spell or effect that also targets one or more allies within 30 feet, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to allow your allies to use your saving throw result or their own, whichever is higher.

##### Tactical Genius (Su) \[1\]

> You gain a bonus teamwork feat.
>
> You must meet all prerequisites for the feat. Any ally within 30 feet of you counts as having that teamwork feat for the purposes of you gaining bonuses from the feat (the ally doesn’t gain the bonus unless it actually has the feat). At the start of each day, you can spend 10 minutes and expend one use of mythic power to replace this teamwork feat with any other teamwork feat you qualify for. You can select this ability up to three times. Each time, you gain an additional bonus teamwork feat. If you have selected this ability more than once, at the start of each day you can replace any number of teamwork feats this ability grants you, spending time and uses of mythic power for each feat you replace.

##### Unwavering Skill (Ex) \[1\]

> You can always take 10 or 20 on checks using class skills, even if [<u>threatened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Threatened-Squares) or in a hazardous situation. You can’t use this ability with skill checks that don’t normally allow you to take 10 or [<u>take 20</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Take-20).

#### 3rd-Tier Marshal Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Aura of Perseverance (Ex) \[1\]

> Your leadership keeps your allies going even when they’re at death’s door. Allies within 30 feet of you don’t become [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious) or [<u>disabled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Disabled) when reduced to 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or fewer. As soon as you and an ally at 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or fewer are more than 30 feet apart, that ally becomes [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious) or [<u>disabled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Disabled). You can expend one use of mythic power to [<u>stabilize</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stable) all [<u>dying</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dying) allies within 30 feet and let all allies within this range ignore the [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered) condition for a number of rounds equal to your tier.

##### Caster’s Friend (Ex) \[1\]

> You bolster the [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) of spellcasting allies standing next to you. A spellcaster who is adjacent to you gains a bonus on [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) checks equal to your tier. Furthermore, if a spellcaster fails a [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) check while adjacent to you, as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to allow that spellcaster to instead automatically succeed at that [<u>concentration</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Concentration) check.

##### Commanding Presence (Ex) \[1\]

> Your force of presence is so overwhelming that others know what you want and can comprehend what you are saying even if they don’t understand the words you speak. You can use your body language and tone of voice alone to ask questions and make statements to those who don’t know your language. When they answer or respond to your communications, you must succeed at a DC 20 [<u>Sense Motive</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sense-motive) check to get the general idea of what they are saying in return. Furthermore, when you use a spell or ability with the [<u>language-dependent descriptor</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Language-Dependent), you can still affect creatures that cannot understand the language you are using, but they must be able to hear you and must have an [<u>Intelligence</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Intelligence-Int-) score of 1 or higher.

##### Concentrated Barrage (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can direct your allies to attack as a unified force. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to enable two allies within 30 feet of you to each make a single ranged or melee attack at a target you designate. Allies must be able to attack the target (either with a ranged weapon or by being close enough to use a melee weapon), and may choose not to.
>
> Each attack is resolved using the attacker’s highest [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus), and this attack doesn’t count toward the ally’s total number of actions on its turn. At 6th tier and higher, you can affect up to three allies with this ability. At 9th tier and higher, you can affect up to four allies with this ability.

##### Confidence (Su) \[1\]

> You can draw upon a deep reserve of confidence in times of need. You can call on confidence three times per day to use the surge mythic ability without expending one use of mythic power. You can select this ability up to three times. Each additional time you select it, you gain three additional uses of confidence per day.

##### Demagogue (Su) \[1\]

> You have an unnatural ability to call and sway crowds with your commanding presence and flawless rhetoric. When in a small town or larger settlement, you can expend one use of mythic power to gather a crowd. Doing so takes 4d10 minutes. Add your tier to your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) bonus and then multiply the sum by 5 to determine how many creatures are in the crowd (as long as the settlement has a sufficient population to provide a crowd of that size, as determined by the GM). For example, a 4th-tier marshal with a +6 bonus to [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) could gather as many as 50 creatures. The crowd remains for 1 hour, and you gain a +10 [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus) on [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) and [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) checks to direct the crowd. You can expend another use of mythic power to extend the effect for another hour. This is a [<u>language-dependent</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Language-Dependent) effect.

##### Dispel Fear (Su) \[1\]

> You can rally your allies and relieve their [<u>fear</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Fear). As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to either end all fear effects affecting allies within 30 feet of you, or end all fear effects on one ally within line of sight who can clearly see you and hear you.

##### Don’t Cross Me (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Even when you’re away from your thralls, they fear and obey you. When you successfully use [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) to make someone helpful, you can expend one use of mythic power to cause the creature to remain helpful to you even in your absence for up to a number of days equal to your tier. At the beginning of each day, the creature may attempt a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save; if it succeeds, this effect ends prematurely (DC equal to the result of your original [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check minus 2 for each day since the creature last had direct contact with you). This is a [<u>mind-affecting</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Mind-Affecting) [<u>fear</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Fear) effect.

##### Glorious Charge (Ex) \[2\]

> You call your allies to make a coordinated [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) at the same time. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) when you start a charge, you can expend one use of mythic power and allow an ally to [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) immediately after your [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) is complete. You can expend additional uses, allowing one more ally to [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) per use spent. An ally’s [<u>charge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Charge) doesn’t count toward the number of actions it can take on its next turn.

##### Inspire Martyrdom (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Your friends and allies are compelled to fulfill your requests, even at the risk of their own safety. When you attempt a [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) check to make a request of someone who has an attitude of helpful toward you, the DC of your [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) check doesn’t increase when you request dangerous aid or request aid that could result in punishment for your target. You can expend one use of mythic power to use this ability when attempting [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) checks, effectively allowing you to coerce someone into performing obviously dangerous or harmful acts. (Such use of this ability is often an evil act.) You must have the persuasive countenance marshal path ability to take this ability.

##### Master of Mercy (Su) \[2\]

> Your [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) provides great relief. If you have the [<u>mercy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Mercy-Su-) class feature, when you [<u>lay on hands</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Lay-On-Hands-Su-), you can expend one use of mythic power to apply all the mercies of your level or lower instead of just the mercies you’ve selected. If you don’t have the [<u>mercy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Mercy-Su-) class feature, you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) to remove a single condition from a creature you [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks), using your tier as your [<u>paladin</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin) level to determine what mercies are available to you. You can select a different mercy each time you use this ability.

##### Master of Shadows (Ex) \[1\]

> You are a master of moving unseen and unheard, and adept at guiding others in quiet and undetected movement. When you or any ally within 30 feet attempts [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) checks, each of you rolls twice and takes the higher result. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to add your tier to one ally’s next [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) check. The ally must be within 30 feet and the check must occur within the next minute.

##### Mob Ruler (Sp) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can move a crowd to tranquility or whip it into a frenzy. By expending one use of mythic power, you can affect a large group of non-mythic creatures—anyone within 20 feet per tier who can see or hear you—with either [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-) or [*<u>calm emotions</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/calm-emotions) ([<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) equal to your character level). If you expend two uses of mythic power, you can escalate an already riotous crowd into [*<u>confusion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/confusion) (as the spell) or direct their ire toward a target of your choosing (as mass [*<u>suggestion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/suggestion)).

##### Mythic Bond (Ex) \[2\]

> Your hunter’s bond ability is more powerful than most. If your bond is with an [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal), that [<u>animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Animal) gains half your [<u>favored enemy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger#TOC-Favored-Enemy-Ex-) bonuses. If your hunter’s bond is with your companions, you can [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) the bond as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), granting your allies your full [<u>favored enemy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/ranger#TOC-Favored-Enemy-Ex-) bonus instead of just half.

##### Shared Alertness (Ex) \[1\]

> Your presence and guidance allow others to avoid traps and other dangers. Allies within 30 feet of you that can clearly see and hear you gain your [<u>trap sense</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Trap-Sense-Ex-) ability. If you have [<u>evasion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Evasion-Ex-) or [<u>improved evasion</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Improved-Evasion-Ex-), you can expend one use of your mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to grant that ability to one ally within 30 feet until the beginning of your next turn.

##### Shatter Resilience (Ex) \[1\]

> You can channel your mythic power through your strike to lower your target’s [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction). When you hit a creature that has [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-), you can expend one use of mythic power as part of the attack to lower the target’s [<u>DR</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Damage-Reduction-Ex-or-Su-) by an amount equal to your tier (to a minimum of 0) for 1 minute. Multiple uses of this ability on the same creature don’t stack.

##### Words of Hope (Su) \[1\]

> As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to inspire allies within a 30-foot radius, granting each of them the ability to roll twice and take the higher result on one [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll), saving throw, skill check, or ability check. An ally can use this ability once per round for a number of rounds equal to half your tier, and must choose whether to roll twice before rolling the die. This is a [<u>language-dependent</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Language-Dependent) emotion effect.

#### 6th-Tier Marshal Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Beacon of Hope (Su)

> You radiate a supernatural aura that fills your allies with hope and resolve. All allies within 30 feet gain a +2 [<u>morale bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Morale-Bonus) against fear and emotion effects, and a +4 bonus on stabilization checks. Furthermore, each time one of these allies is subject to a [<u>healing</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Healing) spell or effect, it heals a number of additional [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) equal to your tier. Once per day as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power and end this aura to heal all allies within 30 feet of an amount of damage equal to 1d6 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) per tier you possess. If dismissed, the aura automatically reactivates 24 hours later.

##### Castigate (Su)

> While your words can soothe and aid your friends, they are equally adept at assaulting your foes. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to unleash a scathing verbal assault that demoralizes all opponents within 30 feet for 1 minute, as if you had successfully used the [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) skill. These opponents must be able to clearly see and hear you, but this ability is not language dependent.

##### Fast Friends (Su)

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You have such a way with words that you can befriend entire groups of people with a single utterance. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to improve the attitudes of all non-mythic creatures within 30 feet by one step. You can use this ability multiple times to improve these creatures’ attitudes by multiple steps. Mythic creatures receive a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save (DC = 10 + your tier + your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier) to negate this effect. The improvement to attitude lasts a number of hours equal to your tier. This ability is generally ineffective in combat (except perhaps to sway the attitude of a crowd of bystanders or an unaffiliated neutral party) or against creatures that intend to harm you and your allies in the immediate future.

##### Fight On (Su)

> With an order or a determined look, you spur a wounded ally to keep fighting. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to allow a creature other than yourself that is reduced to fewer than 0 [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) to remain conscious and alive. The creature can’t die from hit point damage until after the start of your next turn. You can extend this effect for 1 round at the start of your next turn by expending another use of mythic power as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions). Though the creature can’t be killed by hit point damage, any damage it takes still applies to its current [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points). When this ability’s effect ends, the creature falls [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious) or dies if its current [<u>hit points</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Hit-Points) or other circumstances would otherwise cause it to do so. The creature must be within 30 feet for you to start or extend this effect.

##### Inspiring Assault (Su)

> As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to inspire your allies to greatness in combat. Once per turn as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), each of your allies can either move up to its speed or make one melee or ranged attack at its full [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). These actions don’t count against its actions for the turn. Once activated, this ability lasts a number of rounds equal to your tier, but immediately ends if you don’t damage an opponent with an attack or spell on your turn. This ability doesn’t stack with [*<u>haste</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/haste) or similar effects. An ally must be within 30 feet of you at the start of its turn to benefit from this ability.

##### Stones Will Weep (Su)

> **Source** [<u>Pathfinder Player Companion: Mythic Origins</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Your art can [*<u>awaken</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/awaken) the spirits of beasts, plants, and even the elements. You can attempt [<u>Perform</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perform) checks in place of [<u>Handle Animal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/handle-animal) checks. You can expend one use of mythic power to [*<u>speak with animals</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/speak-with-animals) (as [*<u>speak with animals</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/speak-with-animals)), plants (as [*<u>speak with plants</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/speak-with-plants)) and elements (as [*<u>stone tell</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/stone-tell), except this effect targets fire, wind, and water as well as earth) for a number of minutes equal to your tier. While speaking with plants or the elements, you can also request their aid, effectively allowing you to use [*<u>animate objects</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/animate-objects), [*<u>animate plants</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/animate-plants), [*<u>control water</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/control-water), [*<u>control winds</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/c/control-winds), and [*<u>stone shape</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/stone-shape).
>
> You must expend one use of mythic power as well as one use of [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) for each round you want to use these [<u>spell-like abilities</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Spell-Like-Abilities-Sp-), and on each round you can simultaneously use a number of these abilities equal to half your tier. You must have the [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) ability to select this path ability.

##### Surge of Inspiration (Su)

> Your very presence pushes those around you to be their very best. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), when an ally within line of sight misses with an attack or fails a saving throw, you can expend one use of mythic power or your confidence ability to grant the ally the benefits of your surge ability on that [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) or saving throw. The ally uses your surge die, and can apply the bonus after the result is revealed, possibly turning a failure into a success.

##### Unswerving Loyalty (Su)

> You are a bastion of strength and resolve, and you possess the ability to protect your mind from even the most powerful charms and compulsions. You gain [<u>immunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Immunity-Ex-or-Su-) to non-mythic [<u>mind-affecting</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Mind-Affecting) effects, though you can choose to be affected by them if you wish. When subject to a [<u>mind-affecting</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Mind-Affecting) effect from a mythic source, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to gain [<u>immunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Immunity-Ex-or-Su-) to that effect.

##### Words of Valor (Su)

> With a mere speech, you empower your allies to take on any foe. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend two uses of mythic power to inspire all of your allies within a 30-foot radius, granting them the effects of the spell [*<u>heroism</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/heroism) and making them immune to [<u>fear</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Fear) for 1 round per tier. Each mythic creature that is affected by this ability can use its surge ability once during this duration without having to expend one use of mythic power.

#### 1st-Tier Trickster Path Abilities

##### Deadly Throw (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to draw a thrown weapon or alchemical item and make a ranged attack with it. This attack doesn’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). When making a deadly throw, roll twice for the attack, take the higher result, and add your tier to the roll. If the attack misses, the weapon or alchemical item lands adjacent to the target, regardless of the range.

##### Fleet Charge (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to move up to your speed. At any point during this movement, you can make a single melee or ranged attack at your highest [<u>attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus), adding your tier to the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll). This is in addition to any other attacks you make this round. Damage from this attack bypasses all [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Surprise Strike (Ex) \[2\]

As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to make a melee attack or ranged attack against a target within 30 feet, in addition to any other attacks you make this round. When you make a surprise strike, the target is considered [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed) regardless of any class features or abilities it might have, and you add your tier to the [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll). Damage from this attack bypasses [<u>damage reduction</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Damage-Reduction).

##### Aim for the Eye (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can home in on your targets with uncanny and supernatural accuracy. You can make ranged [<u>sneak attacks</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) from up to 60 feet away (rather than 30 feet). When calculating range penalties for ranged attacks, you are always considered to be one range increment closer to the target than you actually are. Whenever you successfully score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits) against a [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed) creature with a ranged attack, as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can expend one use of mythic power to [<u>blind</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Blinded) that creature for 1d4+1 rounds. A mythic creature can attempt a [<u>Fortitude</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Fortitude) save to negate this effect (DC = 10 + your tier + your [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-) modifier). Creatures that are immune to critical hits are immune to this ability, as are creatures with unusual anatomies (GM’s discretion).

##### Assured Drinker (Ex) \[2\]

> No one can stop you from imbibing, even in combat. You don’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) when drinking an elixir, extract, or [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions). As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to retrieve and drink an elixir, extract, or [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions).

##### Assured Skill (Ex) \[1\]

> In times of need, you’re virtually assured success when using skills. As a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) before you roll a skill check, you can expend one use of mythic power to roll that skill check twice, take the higher result, and add your tier to the total.

##### Astounding Disable (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you use [<u>Disable Device</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/disable-device) to disarm a [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) or open a lock, doing so is only a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions) and doesn’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity).

##### Clown (Su) \[1\]

> As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to make one creature laugh uncontrollably, as the [*<u>hideous laughter</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/h/hideous-laughter) spell. The saving throw against this effect is equal to 10 + your tier + your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier. If you aren’t in combat when you use this ability, it can affect any number of creatures in a burst with a radius of 10 feet per tier.

##### Combat Trickery (Ex) \[2\]

> Through buffoonery and deceit, you can trick opponents into moving where you want them. You can make a single [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check using your [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff) check modifier in place of your [<u>CMB</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Bonus). Apply the result of this check to all adjacent opponents, and resolve it for each enemy independently as a [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush), [<u>drag</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Drag), or [<u>reposition</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reposition) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers). For example, if facing three opponents, you can roll one check and attempt to [<u>bull rush</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Bull-Rush) the first, [<u>drag</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Drag) the second, and [<u>reposition</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reposition) the third.

##### Compelling Feint (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Your parries, attacks, and dodges are sporadic and difficult to predict by those who oppose you. Whenever you [<u>feint</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Feint), you can choose to [<u>feint</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Feint) against all opponents within 10 feet, using the same [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff) check for each opponent. If you expend one use of mythic power while you’re feinting, any target you successfully [<u>feint</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Feint) against is considered [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed) against the first attack any creature makes against it before its next turn. You must use this ability after your [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff) check is rolled but before the results are revealed.

##### Control the Mindless (Su) \[1\]

> You can gain control over mindless [<u>constructs</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct) and [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead). Using this ability is a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), and requires you to make a [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) check against the target’s [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense). Use a [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) (arcana) check against a mindless [<u>construct</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct), or a [<u>Knowledge</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/knowledge) (religion) check against a mindless [<u>undead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Undead). If your check equals or exceeds the creature’s [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense), you can give the creature an order that lasts up to 1 round. For every 5 by which you beat the DC, your control lasts 1 additional round. The creature’s creator or controller can’t regain control during this time unless it also uses this ability. If you expend one use of mythic power when attempting to use this ability, the duration of your control increases from rounds to an equal number of minutes.

##### Crime Spree (Su) \[1\]

> As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) when not in combat, you can attempt two [<u>Sleight of Hand</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sleight-of-hand) checks to take items from other creatures, but you take a –5 penalty on each check. As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions) in combat, you can expend one use of mythic power to attempt a [<u>steal</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Steal) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against each creature within your reach that you choose, without provoking [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity).

##### Deadly Dodge (Ex) \[2\]

> As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to gain a +4 [<u>dodge bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Dodge-Bonus) to your AC until the start of your next turn. During this time, whenever a creature misses on a melee attack against you, it provokes an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from you. You can choose to either take this [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) or force that creature to reroll the attack and change the target to a creature of your choice that is adjacent to you and within the attacker’s reach. This reroll uses the same modifiers as the initial [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll).

##### Defensive Move (Ex) \[1\]

> Once per round, you can designate one opponent. Unless you attempt to move through that opponent’s space, your movement (including standing up from a [<u>prone</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Prone) position) doesn’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from that opponent. If you move through the opponent’s space, you can still attempt an [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) check to do so without provoking an [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). You can take this ability more than once. Each additional time you select it, you can designate one additional opponent when you move.

##### Down Like Dominoes (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> When you successfully [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) a foe, as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) you can attempt an additional [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) attack against a creature adjacent to the first foe [<u>tripped</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) at a –4 penalty.
>
> The adjacent creature must be no more than one size category larger than the creature you just [<u>tripped</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip). If this second [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) is successful, you can continue to attempt to [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) creatures as long as they are adjacent to the last one successfully [<u>tripped</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip), taking a cumulative –4 penalty on each [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) attempt after the first.
