In a round, you can [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) a number of creatures equal to half your tier in this way, and only until your first failed [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) attempt. You don’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) for tripping additional foes with this ability, and if you fail a [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) attempt after the first there’s no chance of you tripping yourself.

##### Enhance Magic Items (Ex) \[1\]

> Your mythic presence enhances the power of certain magic items. Add half your tier to the [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) of [*<u>potions</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions), [*<u>scrolls</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/scrolls), staves, and [*<u>wands</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands) you use. When using a staff or [*<u>wand</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/wands), you may [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) the item by expending one use of mythic power instead of one of the item’s charges.

##### Faster than the Eye (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Few can trace your expert skills of legerdemain. You can use [<u>Sleight of Hand</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sleight-of-hand) to lift or plant objects as a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions) with no penalty.
>
> When you use [<u>Sleight of Hand</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sleight-of-hand) to steal something, the target takes a penalty equal to your tier on its [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) check to notice the theft. You can use [<u>Sleight of Hand</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sleight-of-hand) to plant a small object on someone else.
>
> When doing so, you use the same method and bonuses as you would for lifting the same item.

##### Ghostly Performance (Su) \[1\]

> Whenever you use [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance), you don’t need to spend an action to maintain the effect. This means you can maintain the performance if [<u>paralyzed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Paralyzed) or [<u>stunned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned), although it still ends if you are [<u>unconscious</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Unconscious) or [<u>dead</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Dead). You must still expend rounds of [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance) to continue the effect. Once you start a performance, its effect continues whether or not the subjects can perceive you or the performance. When the performance ends, its effects linger for a number of rounds equal to your tier. This extended duration stacks with any other abilities that extend the duration of your performance, such as [<u>Lingering Performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/feats/general-feats/lingering-performance). This ability doesn’t change how many active performances you can have at once.

##### Impeccable Balance (Ex) \[1\]

> Even the smallest of handholds and outcroppings are more than sufficient for your purposes. You automatically succeed at all [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) checks to walk along narrow ledges. Your [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) checks never take a penalty because of obstructed, slippery, sloped, or unsteady surfaces, and you can move at full speed across such surfaces with no penalty. You also gain a +10 bonus to [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense) against [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) attacks.

##### Improbable Prestidigitation (Su) \[1\]

> You can attempt a [<u>Sleight of Hand</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sleight-of-hand) check to hide or retrieve objects in a personal [<u>extradimensional</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Extradimensional-Spaces) space, similar to a glove of storing. You can take 10 or 20 on this check, and the space can hold up to 1 cubic foot of material per tier. An item the size of a [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) or [<u>dagger</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/dagger) can be stored or retrieved with a successful DC 20 [<u>Sleight of Hand</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sleight-of-hand) check; the DC increases to 25 for an item roughly the size of a one-handed weapon. Storing an item is a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), and retrieving it is a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions). As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to retrieve any number of items from your [<u>extradimensional</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Extradimensional-Spaces) space.

##### Inspire Minions (Ex) \[1\]

> When you use a spell, a [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance), or another class feature to grant a morale or [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus), the bonus increases by 2 for creatures with at least 4 [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-) fewer than you. For example, if you’re a 6th-level [<u>bard</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard) using [<u>inspire courage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Inspire-Courage-Su-), creatures with 2 [<u>Hit Dice</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Hit-Dice-HD-) or fewer receive a +4 [<u>morale bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Morale-Bonus) on [<u>fear</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Fear) saves and a +4 [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus) on attack and weapon damage rolls instead of +2.

##### Knot Expert (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can tie knots in rope with a flick of your wrist, and can do so from the far end of the rope. You can tie a knot in any rope you hold, including turning it into a [<u>lasso</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/lasso), as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions). You can also use a [<u>whip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/whip), [<u>lasso</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/lasso), [<u>spiked chain</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/equipment/weapons/weapon-descriptions/chain-spiked), or similar weapon to [<u>constrict</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Constrict-Ex-) an opponent.
>
> In order to do so, you must make a successful attack with the rope-like weapon, after which you can, as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), attempt a [<u>grapple</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Grapple) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) against that creature, adding your tier to your [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check. If you are successful, the creature is considered to be [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled), though you are not, and you can deal [<u>constrict</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/universal-monster-rules#TOC-Constrict-Ex-) damage to the creature (as the universal monster ability) equal to the amount of damage you could normally deal with the weapon used.

##### Master Dilettante (Ex) \[1\]

> You are passingly familiar with almost every subject and have dabbled in numerous fields and activities. You gain a [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus) equal to your tier on all untrained skill checks. You can attempt all skill checks untrained, even if the skill normally cannot be used untrained.

##### Master of Escape (Su) \[1\]

> You can use the [<u>Escape Artist</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/escape-artist) skill to break free from spells and magical effects that limit your movement or actions. While you’re subject to a spell or effect that gives you the [<u>entangled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Entangled), [<u>grappled</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Grappled), [<u>paralyzed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Paralyzed), [<u>pinned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Pinned), [<u>staggered</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Staggered), or [<u>stunned</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Stunned) condition, once per round as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), you can attempt an [<u>Escape Artist</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/escape-artist) check to end the condition. The DC for this check is equal to the save DC of the spell or effect that caused the condition. If you succeed, you end one such condition on yourself (your choice), though this doesn’t make you immune to similar effects from the same source (such as moving through [*<u>black tentacles</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/b/black-tentacles) or [*<u>web</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/w/web)). If the spell or effect doesn’t allow a saving throw, you can’t use this ability to overcome it.

##### Nimble Glide (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you fall, you don’t take any damage and you land on your feet. When using this ability, you fall at a rate of 150 feet per round, and can move up to 5 feet horizontally for every 10 feet you fall.

##### No One of Consequence (Ex) \[1\]

> Your mythic nature is a cloak that can shield you from the minds and memories of others. Creatures struggle to recognize you, recall your appearance, remember the nature of conversations they had with you, and recount the actions you took when you were in their presence. If a creature makes a deliberate attempt to recall details about time spent with you, it must succeed at a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) save (DC 10 + your tier + your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier) to recall anything beyond vague details. In addition, you’re under a permanent [*<u>nondetection</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/n/nondetection) effect with a [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level) equal to your character level plus your tier. You can end this effect at any time, and can resume it as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions).

##### Persuasive Countenance (Su) \[1\]

> Whenever you encounter a creature whose attitude is at least indifferent to you, treat its attitude as one step better. If the creature’s starting attitude is helpful, you can make requests of the creature with a +5 bonus on your [<u>Diplomacy</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/diplomacy) or [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check.

##### Ranged Disable (Ex) \[1\]

> As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can use a thrown weapon or reach weapon to attempt a [<u>Disable Device</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/disable-device) check to disable a device. If you’re using a thrown weapon, the range of this ability is 30 feet; otherwise, it’s your reach with the reach weapon. You take a –5 penalty on the check and can’t take 10 on the check, even if you have an ability that would normally allow you to do so. If you expend one use of mythic power, you ignore the –5 penalty and can attempt the check in place of an attack (such as if making a full attack or [<u>attack of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity)).

##### Redirect Attention (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You can expend one use of mythic power to direct the attention of a number of creatures equal to your tier within line of sight toward a location or general direction you choose—and away from others. Non-mythic creatures automatically turn toward the point you choose. Mythic targets receive a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) saving throw to resist this effect (DC = 10 + your tier + your [<u>Wisdom</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Wisdom-Wis-) modifier). The effect persists for 1 round per tier, during which time affected targets receive a +5 bonus on [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) checks to notice anything unusual within a 60-foot-cone in the direction you indicate and a –15 penalty on [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) checks to notice anything elsewhere. This effect counts as a diversion to hide for creatures attempting to use [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) in any area other than the location you directed attention toward.

##### Ricochet (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever a creature has [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover) from your ranged attack, you can deflect the shot off a nearby surface, treating that surface as the origin point of the attack to determine whether the target receives a [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover) bonus to its AC. You can use this ability to attack a creature with total [<u>concealment</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Concealment) from you if you know what square it occupies, but the attack still has a 50% miss chance.

##### Shadow Stealth (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you’re in darkness, you can move at full speed within that area without taking a penalty on [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) checks. As a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to [*<u>teleport</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/teleport) from one area of darkness to another within 100 feet as if using greater [*<u>teleport</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/teleport). You must have line of effect to the destination.

##### Sniper’s Riposte (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You’re an expert at defending yourself while aiming a ranged weapon. You don’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity) from making ranged attacks. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to negate the [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) check penalty for sniping for 1 round.

##### Subtle Magic (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you cast a spell or [<u>activate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Activate-Magic-Item) a spell trigger or spell completion magic item, you can attempt a [<u>Bluff</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/bluff) skill check opposed by a Sense Motive check from anyone that can see you. Those who fail the opposed check don’t know that you cast a spell or activated an item unless it has an obvious effect that emanates from you. In addition, the magical auras of magical effects on you and magic items you carry (except for artifacts) can’t be detected.

##### Supreme Stealth (Ex) \[1\]

> Choose one of the following senses: [<u>blindsight</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Blindsight-and-Blindsense), [<u>scent</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Scent), or [<u>tremorsense</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/monsters-foes/#Tremorsense). A creature using the chosen sense can’t automatically detect you, and must succeed at [<u>Perception</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/perception) checks as normal to do so. If you choose [<u>blindsight</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Blindsight-and-Blindsense), this ability also affects [<u>blindsense</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/special-abilities#TOC-Blindsight-and-Blindsense). You can select this ability up to three times, each time choosing a different sense.

##### This Might Just Work (Ex) \[1\]

> You’re adept at combining optimistic resourcefulness with natural skill. You can attempt a skill check with a class skill in place of a skill that uses the same ability score modifier. For example, you could attempt an [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) check in place of a [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth) check because both skills are based on [<u>Dexterity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Dexterity-Dex-). You take a –5 penalty on this check and can’t take 10 or 20 on it. The GM can disallow certain uses of this ability when the skill can’t possibly be applied to the task.

##### Thwart Detection (Su) \[1\]

> You can bypass magical [<u>constructs</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct), spells, and traps that are meant to allow only certain creatures to move or act within their range. This includes [<u>constructs</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct), spells, and traps that can be bypassed with a password as well as those that can be programmed to exclude specific creatures based on type or other physical characteristics (such as [*<u>alarm</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/a/alarm), [*<u>forbiddance</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/f/forbiddance), [*<u>glyph of warding</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/g/glyph-of-warding), and [*<u>symbol of death</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/symbol-of-death)). You must be aware of the [<u>construct</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct), spell, or [<u>trap’s</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) existence, though not necessarily its specific parameters, for this ability to function. For instance, you can’t bypass a [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) you haven’t noticed. You can fool a mindless [<u>construct</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/bestiary/rules-for-monsters/creature-types#TOC-Construct) if it was programmed to allow certain creatures to enter its area without provoking a response, but not if it was ordered to prevent anyone from entering with no exceptions. If you expend one use of mythic power for each effect, you can bring with you a number of allies equal to your tier. They must remain within 10 feet of you until out of range of the bypassed effect.

##### Titan’s Bane (Ex) \[1\]

> You can move through the space of any creature two or more size categories larger than you without provoking [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity), and you can share such a creature’s space. When sharing a larger opponent’s space, you gain [<u>cover</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Cover) against all melee and ranged attacks it makes, and it is considered [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed) for the purposes of any melee or ranged attacks you make against it.

##### Transfer Magic (Su) \[1\]

> Your mythic nature allows you to take magic from others as easily as you could take their gold. By expending one use of mythic power as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can make a melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) to transfer an active magical effect from a target creature to you. If you succeed, the highest-level effect on the target transfers to you (determine randomly if the target has multiple effects with the same level), ending the effect for the target and continuing it on you with the remaining duration as if you were the original target. You may end the effect on yourself as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions); this doesn’t cause it to revert to the original target. If the transferred magic can’t affect you (for example, if it doesn’t affect creatures of your type), it ends immediately as if dispelled. You can’t use this ability to transfer continuous bonuses from magic items, such as an [<u>armor bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Armor-Bonus) from bracers of armor.

##### Trap Taker (Ex) \[1\]

> You can use the [<u>Use Magic Device</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/use-magic-device) skill instead of [<u>Disable Device</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/disable-device) to disable magical traps. If your [<u>Use Magic Device</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/use-magic-device) check overcomes the DC to disable the [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) by 5 or more, you can instead leave the [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) in place, but change it so that you and your allies can bypass it. You can also alter the [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) so the original creator and any creatures he might have designated as safe instead set off the [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/). If you expend one use of mythic power when altering a [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) in this way, you can move the [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) to any point within 100 feet that is in line of sight.

##### Treacherous Critical (Ex) \[1\]

> When you score a critical threat, you can attempt a free [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check instead of dealing extra damage. If you do, attempt your confirmation roll as normal and add your tier to the result. If the result is equal to or greater than your opponent’s [<u>CMD</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Defense), the attack deals damage as normal and you can attempt a free [<u>dirty trick</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Dirty-Trick), [<u>disarm</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Disarm), [<u>reposition</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Reposition), or [<u>trip</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Trip) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check against that opponent, using the confirmation roll as your [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) check for the purpose of determining the effects of the maneuver.

##### Unintentional Feint (Ex) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> You make near misses look like expert feints, and your efforts are rarely wasted. If you make an [<u>attack roll</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Roll) that misses your original target, you can expend one use of mythic power to specify a second target, which must be adjacent to you (if a melee attack) or the original target (if a ranged attack). If the second target would be hit by what you rolled, you hit that target and deal damage as normal.

##### Unwavering Skill (Ex) \[1\]

> You can always take 10 or 20 on class skills, even if [<u>threatened</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Threatened-Squares) or in a hazardous situation. You can’t use this ability with skill checks that don’t normally allow you to take 10 or [<u>take 20</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Take-20).

##### Wall Run (Ex) \[1\]

> When you move, you can travel across vertical surfaces as if they were floors. If your movement doesn’t end on a horizontal surface, you fall at the end of your move unless you are able to remain in place using another ability (such as using the [<u>Climb</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/climb) skill to cling to a handhold). The vertical surface must be capable of supporting your weight—for example, you couldn’t move across a windowpane or curtain.

#### 3rd-Tier Trickster Path Abilities

You must be at least 3rd tier to select these path abilities.

##### Combat Saboteur (Ex) \[1\]

> You can sabotage an opponent’s gear with a simple touch. This sabotage is a [<u>sunder</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Sunder) [<u>combat maneuver</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuvers) that doesn’t provoke [<u>attacks of opportunity</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attacks-of-Opportunity). Add your tier to your [<u>CMB</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Combat-Maneuver-Bonus) when attempting this maneuver. If you succeed, you can give the [<u>broken</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Broken) condition to a single item the opponent is wielding or carrying. This attack doesn’t deal any damage to the object. The condition can be removed by spending 1 minute undoing the sabotage, or instantaneously with [*<u>mending</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mending) or a similar spell.

##### Critical Skill (Su) \[1\]

> Whenever you roll a natural 20 on a skill check, you perform that skill with exceptional proficiency. Typically, this means that the task takes half the normal amount of time to accomplish. If the skill would normally take a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), it instead takes a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions). If it would normally take a standard or [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions), it instead takes a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions). If it would normally take a swift or [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions), it instead takes no time at all. The GM might rule that instead of reducing the amount of time needed, using the skill takes the normal amount of time but the critical success results in some other added benefit, depending on the skill being used and the situation.

##### Ethereal Trapsmith (Su) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to spontaneously create a mechanical [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) in any empty space within 30 feet. This [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) must be chosen from those listed on the traps page. The [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/)‘s CR can’t be higher than your tier, and the [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) remains in the place you designate for a number of hours equal to your tier. As long as you have line of sight to a [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) that you’ve summoned with this ability, created through other means, bypassed, or reprogrammed to your benefit, you can trigger that [<u>trap</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/traps-hazards-and-special-terrains/traps/) as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), no matter your distance to it.

##### Feather Step (Su) \[1\]

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Your steps are deft and always sure.
>
> You gain a bonus on [<u>Acrobatics</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/acrobatics) checks equal to your tier. You can move at full speed across any narrow or uneven surface, and can run across fragile surfaces that can’t hold your weight (including liquids) without peril as long as you end your turn on a solid surface. You leave no tracks (as [*<u>pass without trace</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/p/pass-without-trace)), and ignore adverse effects from non-magical difficult terrain and ground hazards, including caltrops. As an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to balance on razor edges and pinpoints, allowing you to ignore damage from [*<u>spiked pit</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/spiked-pit) traps and similar hazards for a number of rounds equal to your tier.

##### Fickle Attack (Ex) \[2\] \[1\] (primero 2, después 1 si volves a tomarla)

> Whenever you roll damage for a melee or ranged attack with a weapon or alchemical item, you can treat any natural 1s on the damage dice as if they were the highest possible number on those dice. You can select this ability up to three times. The second time you select it, treat 1s and 2s as the highest value. The third time, treat 1s, 2s, and 3s as the highest value.

##### Menacing Whisper (Ex) \[1\]

> Whenever you’re adjacent to a creature that can’t see you (whether through [<u>Stealth</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/stealth), magic, or the creature being unable to see) but can hear you, as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) you can attempt an [<u>Intimidate</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/intimidate) check to demoralize that creature. You can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>free action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Free-Actions) to give such a creature a [*<u>suggestion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/s/suggestion). The save DC against this effect is 10 + your tier + your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier.

##### Mirror Dodge (Su) \[2\]

> When hit by a melee or ranged attack, you can expend one use of mythic power as an [<u>immediate action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Immediate-Actions) to replace yourself with an illusory duplicate and [*<u>teleport</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/t/teleport) to any open square within 30 feet of your current position. You take no damage from this attack, which instead destroys your illusory duplicate (similar to [*<u>mirror image</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/m/mirror-image)). Using this ability requires a line of effect to the [<u>teleportation</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Conjuration-Teleportation) target square.

##### Perfect Lie (Ex) \[1\]

> When telling a lie, you can expend one use of mythic power to make the lie indiscernible from the truth by both [<u>Sense Motive</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/sense-motive) and magic. Obvious proof of your falsehood still reveals the lie for what it is, but in absence of proof, those who hear your lie believe it.

##### Perfect Mimic (Su) \[1\]

> You can use the [<u>Disguise</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/disguise) skill as a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions). When you attempt to impersonate a specific person, those familiar with the individual receive only half the normal bonus from familiarity to see through your disguise. If you expend one use of mythic power when creating a disguise to impersonate an individual, you can read that creature’s mind as if using [*<u>detect thoughts</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/detect-thoughts) with a range of 1 mile. This allows you to better mimic the individual, granting you a further +10 bonus on opposed [<u>Disguise</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/skills/disguise) skill checks. The target of your impersonation receives a [<u>Will</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Will) saving throw (DC 10 + your tier + your [<u>Charisma</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/ability-scores#TOC-Charisma-Cha-) modifier) to negate the [*<u>detect thoughts</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/d/detect-thoughts) aspect of this ability.

##### Sardonic Wit (Su) \[1\]

> You know exactly when to lighten the mood and help shake off the doldrums. As a [<u>move action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Move-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power to remove a single [<u>mind-affecting</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Magic-Descriptor-Mind-Affecting) effect from one creature within 30 feet. This ability doesn’t work on effects with an instantaneous or permanent duration.

##### Vanishing Move (Su) \[2\]

> When you wish to not be seen, you aren’t. As a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions), you can make yourself [<u>invisible</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Invisible) until the end of your turn. This effect ends if you do anything other than move. If you expend one use of mythic power when using this ability, it instead acts as greater [*<u>invisibility</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic/all-spells/i/invisibility) using double your tier as your [<u>caster level</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic#TOC-Caster-Level).

#### 6th-Tier Trickster Path Abilities

You must be at least 6th tier to select these path abilities.

##### Bloody Streak (Ex)

> **Source** [<u>PPC:MO</u>](http://www.amazon.com/gp/product/1601255691/ref=as_li_qf_sp_asin_il_tl?ie=UTF8&camp=1789&creative=9325&creativeASIN=1601255691&linkCode=as2&tag=httpwwwd20pfs-20)
>
> Whenever you successfully deal [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage to a creature, you can expend one use of mythic power as a [<u>swift action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Swift-Actions) to heighten your bloodshed. Thereafter, every consecutive round that you deal [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage, add a cumulative +1d6 bonus on your [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage roll. The targets of these [<u>sneak attacks</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) don’t need to be the same. You can gain a maximum number of bonus d6s on your [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage in this way equal to your tier. As soon as you fail to deal [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage to a creature in a round, this effect ends and you no longer gain the bonus on [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage until you use this ability again. For example, a 10th-level [<u>rogue</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue), 6th-tier trickster uses this ability after dealing 5d6 points of [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage to a foe. On the next round, she deals 6d6 points of [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage to a foe (5d6 from her [<u>rogue</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue) levels + 1d6 from this ability). The round after that, she deals 7d6 points of [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage, but on the next round, she fails to deal any [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage, and so her [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage resets back to 5d6.

##### Class Mimic (Su)

> The abilities of your allies are available to you with a [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks). As a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can expend one use of mythic power and [<u>touch</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) a willing creature to copy one of that creature’s class features. You gain the use of this class feature for 10 minutes per tier, using the level of the creature touched as your level for any effects based on level. You can’t copy an ability that has a limited number of uses per day (such as [<u>rage</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/barbarian#TOC-Rage-Ex-), [<u>smite evil</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/paladin#TOC-Smite-Evil-Su-), or spellcasting) or an ability that involves a companion (such as [<u>eidolon</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/base-classes/summoner#TOC-Eidolon), hunter’s bond, or [<u>nature bond</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/druid#TOC-Nature-Bond-Ex-)). You can have only one use of this ability in effect at one time. If you use this ability again, any previous use immediately ends. You can’t copy the same ability more than once per day.

##### Enduring Elixir (Su)

> Whenever you drink an elixir, extract, or [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) with a duration of 10 minutes per level or longer, you can change its duration to 1 day. You can have this ability in effect on only one elixir, extract, or [*<u>potion</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) at a time. Using it on a second one causes the duration of the first to revert to normal—and to end immediately if the normal duration would have expired. If you’re at least 9th tier, you can select this ability a second time to have it work on extracts and [*<u>potions</u>*](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/magic-items/potions) with a duration of 1 minute or longer.

##### Precision Critical (Ex)

> Whenever you score a [<u>critical hit</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Critical-Hits), double any extra precision damage dice, such as [<u>sneak attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/rogue#TOC-Sneak-Attack) damage. These dice are only doubled, not multiplied by the weapon’s critical modifier.

##### Slayer’s Cyclone (Ex)

> When you spot a breach in an enemy’s defenses, you make attacks with a lethal blend of speed and precision. As a [<u>full-round action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Full-Round-Actions), you can make a single melee attack at your highest [<u>base attack bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Attack-Bonus). If you hit and the target is [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed), deal damage as normal. You can then move and make another attack. You may continue this process until you miss, attack an opponent that isn’t [<u>flat-footed</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/conditions#TOC-Flat-Footed), or attack an opponent you already hit this round. You can’t move more than double your speed in this way in 1 round.

##### Steal Power (Su)

> By attempting a melee [<u>touch attack</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Touch-Attacks) as a [<u>standard action</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/gamemastering/combat#TOC-Standard-Actions), you can try to steal mythic power from another creature. The target must have at least one use of mythic power available. If the attack hits, attempt an opposed d20 roll against the target, adding your tier. If your total exceeds that of the target, the target loses one use of mythic power and you gain one use. You can use this ability on a given creature only once per day.

##### Unending Performance (Su)

> You can imbue an ally with power that lingers for a full day. Whenever you use a spell, [<u>bardic performance</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/classes/core-classes/bard#TOC-Bardic-Performance), or another class feature to grant a morale or [<u>competence bonus</u>](https://www.d20pfsrd.com/basics-ability-scores/glossary#TOC-Competence-Bonus), you can designate any one creature affected by this bonus. The designated creature keeps this bonus for up to 24 hours. You can have only one creature designated in this way at a time. If you designate a new creature, the bonus immediately ends for the previous target. The creature you designate must be one of the targets when you cast the spell, start the performance, or use the class feature.

TEMPLAR

REQUIREMENTS:

Alignment: Any Lawful

Base Attack Bonus: +4

Skills: Intimidate 3 Ranks, Knowledge Arcane 2 ranks, Knowledge Planes 2 ranks.

Special: Consumo mensual de Lyrium.

CLASS SKILLS:

Intimidate, Knowledge Arcana, Knowledge Planes, Percepction, Sense Motive.

CLASS FEATURES:

level bab fort save ref save will save special

1: 1 1 1 1 Smite Magic 1/day, Lyrium Adict.

2: 2 1 1 1 Disruptive, Dispel.

3: 3 2 2 2 Smite Magic 2/day, Master Dispeler, Immediate Dispel.

4: 4 2 2 2 Spell Resistance, Spellbreaker.

5: 5 3 3 3 Smite Magic 3/day

6: 6 3 3 3 Will Evasion, fort evasion.

7: 7 4 4 4 Smite Magic 4/day, Greater Dispel.

8: 8 4 4 4 Greater Spell Resistance.

9: 9 5 5 5 Smite Magic 5/day

10: 10 5 5 5 Greater Will evasion, greater fort evasion, mythic Dispel.

Smite Magic (Su): This ability functions as the paladin’s smite evil class feature but against Magic Users (any creature that has a caster level).

Lyrium Adict (Su): Necesitan consumir lyrium para que sus habilidades funcionen. Una dosis mensual, cara y muy dificil de conseguir sin ser parte de la iglesia. Si no la consumen, tienen -4 Will saves permanente, y restear no les recupera usos de sus habilidades de clase.

Disruptive (Feat): Your training makes it difficult for enemy spellcasters to safely cast spells near you.

Benefit: The DC to cast spells defensively increases by +4 for all enemies that are within your threatened area. This increase to casting spells defensively only applies if you are aware of the enemy's location and are capable of taking an attack of opportunity. If you can only take one attack of opportunity per round and have already used that attack, this increase does not apply.

Dispel (Su): As Dispel Magic but you use double your Templar lvl instead of your caster level.

Master Dispeler (Su): +1 to dispel check at lvl 3, and every 2 lvls after.

Immediate Dispel (Su): can use dispel as an immediate action, instead of a standard.

Spell Resistance (Su): You gain 5 + double your templar level as Spell Resistance.

Spellbreaker (feat): You can strike at enemy spellcasters who fail to cast defensively when you threaten them.

Benefit: Enemies in your threatened area that fail their checks to cast spells defensively provoke attacks of opportunity from you.

Will Evasion (Su): as evasion but for will saves xd

Fort Evasion (Su): as evasion but for fort saves xd

Greater Dispel (Su): As Greater Dispel Magic but you use double your Templar lvl instead of your caster level.

Greater Spell Resistance (Su): Your spell resistance goes up to 10 + double your templar level, instead of 5.

Greater will evasion (Su): as improved evasion but for will saves xd

Greater fort evasion (Su): as improved evasion but for fort saves xd

Mythic Dispel (Su): As mythic dispel but instead of healing yourself, you damage the caster of the spell if its nearby.

BLOOD MAGE

Blood Magic Adept \[Feat\] (Requisitos: caster level 3): 1d2 con damage por caster level/dc agregado, hasta +3 caster level a ese spell (stackea con todo) o DC. Provoca adiccion psicologica, requiriendo posibles will saves (o tiradas de determinacion) para no querer usarlo otra vez al siguiente turno. Fallar muchas saves seguidas en una situacion desesperante puede llevar a convertirse en abominacion que es basicamente morir.

Blood Magic Expert \[Feat\] (Requisitos: 3era esfera): Hasta +4 caster level de maximo.

Blood Magic Master \[Feat\] (Requisitos: 5ta esfera): Permite cambiar 2 caster level por +1 damage per die (que tambien stackea con todo). Con damage es siempre el minimo.

GREY WARDEN

Grey Warden \[Feat\]: Felicidades, te vas a morir en 2 o 3 decadas. Ganas pesadillas que te dan estres. Ah pero tenes inmunidad politica te dejan pasar a todas partes asi seas un apostata o tu vieja en tanga. +1 percep para detectar darkspawn, cada mes que pases siendo grey warden esto crece hasta un +10 maximo. Los darkspawn tambien tienen +5 percep para detectarte a vos. Esta percepcion no se ve limitada por nada, como si fuese blindsense.

Master Warden \[Feat\]: Si no tenes favored enemy, lo ganas a la mitad, en base a tu nivel, vs darkspawn. Ganas Blindsense 15ft vs darkspawn.

DIVINE MAGIC

Existe pero no existe, seria solo otra forma de hacer magia, la magia es bastante unica y reinterpretable en el universo de dragon age, en el caso de la magia divina en la mayoria de casos seria basicamente ligada a los espiritus del fade. Digamos podrias ser un cleric que "su dios" seria ponele un espiritu de la justicia. y eso le daria domains razonables y acordes a tal tipo de espiritu.

Andraste no da poderes.

the maker no da poderes.

Ser divine caster sigue estando atado a las mismas reglas que cualquier otro caster, sos paria o sos del circulo.
